#ifndef __N32WB033_H__
#define __N32WB033_H__

#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C" {
#endif


/**  N32WB033_Library_Basic **/

#if !defined USE_STDPERIPH_DRIVER
/*
 * Comment the line below if you will not use the peripherals drivers.
   In this case, these drivers will not be included and the application code will
   be based on direct access to peripherals registers
   */
#define USE_STDPERIPH_DRIVER
#endif

/*
 * In the following line adjust the value of External High Speed oscillator (HSE)
   used in your application

   Tip: To avoid modifying this file each time you need to use different HSE, you
        can define the HSE value in your toolchain compiler preprocessor.
  */
#if !defined HSE_VALUE
#define HSE_VALUE (32000000U) /* Value of the External oscillator in Hz */
#endif                       /* HSE_VALUE */

/** In the following line adjust the HSE\HSI\LSI\LSE Startup
   Timeout value.  The user shall keep only the clocks actually present on
   the target chip and remove the rest. **/
#define HSE_STARTUP_TIMEOUT ((uint16_t)0x1000U)     /* Time out for HSE start up */
#define HSI_STARTUP_TIMEOUT ((uint16_t)0x0300U)     /* Time out for HSI start up */
#define LSI_STARTUP_TIMEOUT ((uint16_t)0x0300U)     /* Time out for LSI start up */
#define LSE_STARTUP_TIMEOUT ((uint32_t)0x00C00000U) /* Time out for LSE start up */

#define HSI_VALUE (64000000U) /* Value of the Internal oscillator in Hz*/
#define LSI_VALUE (32000U)   /* Value of the Internal Low Speed oscillator in Hz*/
#define LSE_VALUE (32768U)   /* Value of the External Low Speed oscillator in Hz*/

/** N32WB033 Standard Peripheral Library version number **/
#define __N32WB033_STDPERIPH_VERSION_MAIN   (0x01U) /* [31:24] main version */
#define __N32WB033_STDPERIPH_VERSION_SUB1   (0x00U) /* [23:16] sub1 version */
#define __N32WB033_STDPERIPH_VERSION_SUB2   (0x00U) /* [15:8]  sub2 version */
#define __N32WB033_STDPERIPH_VERSION_RC     (0x00U) /* [7:0]  release candidate */
#define __N32WB033_STDPERIPH_VERSION        ( (__N32WB033_STDPERIPH_VERSION_MAIN << 24U)\
                                                 |(__N32WB033_STDPERIPH_VERSION_SUB1 << 16U)\
                                                 |(__N32WB033_STDPERIPH_VERSION_SUB2 << 8U)\
                                                 |(__N32WB033_STDPERIPH_VERSION_RC))

/** Configuration of the Cortex-M0 Processor and Core Peripherals **/
#define __MPU_PRESENT             1U /* MPU not present by default; set to 1 on Cortex-M0+ parts with MPU */
#define __VTOR_PRESENT						1U /* set to 1 on Cortex-M0+ parts with VTOR */
#define __NVIC_PRIO_BITS          2U /* N32WB033 uses 2 Bits for the Priority Levels */
#define __Vendor_SysTickConfig    0U /* Set to 1 if different SysTick Config is used */

/** N32WB033 Interrupt Number Definition **/

typedef enum IRQn
{
    /** Cortex-M0 Processor Exceptions Numbers **/
    NonMaskableInt_IRQn         = -14,    /* 2 Non Maskable Interrupt                                */
    HardFault_IRQn              = -13,    /* 3 Cortex-M0 Hard Fault Interrupt                        */
    SVC_IRQn                    = -5,     /* 11 Cortex-M0 SV Call Interrupt                          */
    PendSV_IRQn                 = -2,     /* 14 Cortex-M0 Pend SV Interrupt                          */
    SysTick_IRQn                = -1,     /* 15 Cortex-M0 System Tick Interrupt                      */

    /** N32WB033 specific Interrupt Numbers **/
    WWDG_IRQn                    = 0,  /* Window WatchDog Interrupt */
    BLE_SW_IRQn                  = 1,  /* BLE SW Trigger Interrupt */
    RTC_IRQn                     = 2,  /* RTC Interrupt through EXTI Line16/17 */
    BLE_HSLOT_IRQn               = 3,  /* BLE Half Slot Reference Interrupt */
    Reserved                     = 4,  /* Reserved */
    RCC_IRQn                     = 5,  /* RCC Global Interrupt */
    EXTI0_1_IRQn                 = 6,  /* EXTI Line0 ~ Line1 Interrupt */
    EXTI2_3_IRQn                 = 7,  /* EXTI Line2 ~ Line3 Interrupt */
    EXTI4_15_IRQn                = 8,  /* EXTI Line4 ~ Line15 Interrupt */
    BLE_FINETGT_IRQn             = 9,  /* BLE Fine Time Target Interrupt */
    BLE_FIFO_IRQn                = 10, /* BLE FIFO Interrupt */
    DMA_CH1_2_3_4_IRQn           = 11, /* DMA Channel1/2/3/4 Interrupt */
    DMA_CH5_6_7_IRQn             = 12, /* DMA Channel5/6/7 Interrupt */
    ATIM1_BRK_UP_TRG_COM_IRQn    = 13, /* ATIM1 Break, Update, Trigger and Commutation Interrupt */
    ATIM1_CC_IRQn                = 14, /* ATIM1 Capture Compare Interrupt */
    LPTIM_IRQn                   = 15, /* LPTIM Global Interrupt through EXTI Line20 */
    GTIM1_IRQn                   = 16, /* GTIM1 Global Interrupt */
    BLE_ERROR_CYPT_IRQn          = 17, /* BLE Error/Crypt Interrupt */
    BLE_WAKE_IRQn                = 18, /* BLE Wakeup Interrupt through EXTI Line21 */
    BLE_TIMESTAMP_TGT1_IRQn      = 19, /* BLE Timestamp Target1 Interrupt */
    BTIM1_IRQn                   = 20, /* BTIM1 Global Interrupt */
    ADC_IRQn                     = 21, /* ADC Global Interrupt */
    SPI2_SPI3_IRQn               = 22, /* SPI2/SPI3 Global Interrupt */
    I2C1_I2C2_IRQn               = 23, /* I2C1/I2C2 Global Interrupt */
    BLE_TIMESTAMP_TGT2_IRQn      = 24, /* BLE Timestamp Target2 Interrupt */
    SPI1_IRQn                    = 25, /* SPI1 Global Interrupt */
    BLE_SLP_IRQn                 = 26, /* BLE Sleep Mode Interrupt */
    LPUART2_IRQn                 = 27, /* LPUART2 Global Interrupt through EXTI Line19 */
    USART1_IRQn                  = 28, /* USART1 Global Interrupt */
    LPUART1_IRQn                 = 29, /* LPUART1 Global Interrupt through EXTI Line18 */
    USART2_IRQn                  = 30, /* USART2 Global Interrupt */
    CAN_IRQn                     = 31, /* CAN Global Interrupt */
} IRQn_Type;

#include "core_cm0plus.h"
#include "system_n32wb033.h"
#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdbool.h>

/* N32WB033 Standard Peripheral Library old types (maintained for legacy purpose) */
typedef int32_t  s32;
typedef int16_t s16;
typedef int8_t  s8;

typedef const int32_t sc32;  /* Read Only */
typedef const int16_t sc16;  /* Read Only */
typedef const int8_t sc8;    /* Read Only */

typedef __IO int32_t  vs32;
typedef __IO int16_t  vs16;
typedef __IO int8_t   vs8;

typedef __I int32_t vsc32;  /* Read Only */
typedef __I int16_t vsc16;  /* Read Only */
typedef __I int8_t vsc8;   /* Read Only */

typedef uint32_t  u32;
typedef uint16_t u16;
typedef uint8_t  u8;

typedef const uint32_t uc32;  /* Read Only */
typedef const uint16_t uc16;  /* Read Only */
typedef const uint8_t uc8;   /* Read Only */

typedef __IO uint32_t  vu32;
typedef __IO uint16_t vu16;
typedef __IO uint8_t  vu8;

typedef __I uint32_t vuc32;  /* Read Only */
typedef __I uint16_t vuc16;  /* Read Only */
typedef __I uint8_t vuc8;   /* Read Only */

typedef enum {RESET = 0, SET = 1} FlagStatus, INTStatus;

typedef enum {DISABLE = 0, ENABLE = 1} FunctionalState;

typedef enum {ERROR = 0, SUCCESS = 1} ErrorStatus;

/* N32WB033 Standard Peripheral Library old definitions (maintained for legacy purpose) */
#define HSEStartUp_TimeOut   HSE_STARTUP_TIMEOUT
#define HSE_Value            HSE_VALUE
#define HSI_Value            HSI_VALUE


#define PERIPH_BASE          ((uint32_t)0x40000000U)
#define APB1PERIPH_BASE      ((uint32_t)(PERIPH_BASE))
#define APB2PERIPH_BASE      ((uint32_t)(PERIPH_BASE + (uint32_t)0x10000U))
#define AHBPERIPH_BASE       ((uint32_t)(PERIPH_BASE + (uint32_t)0x20000U))

#define PATCH_BASE           ((uint32_t)(AHBPERIPH_BASE + (uint32_t)0x2000U))
#define MODEM_BASE           ((uint32_t)(AHBPERIPH_BASE + (uint32_t)0x2400U))
#define BASEBAND_REG_BASE    ((uint32_t)(AHBPERIPH_BASE + (uint32_t)0x8000U))
#define EXCHANGE_MEM_BASE    ((uint32_t)(AHBPERIPH_BASE + (uint32_t)0xC000U))  


/** Bit Offset register **/
#define REG_BIT1_OFFSET    ((uint32_t)0x00000001U)
#define REG_BIT2_OFFSET    ((uint32_t)0x00000002U)
#define REG_BIT3_OFFSET    ((uint32_t)0x00000003U)
#define REG_BIT4_OFFSET    ((uint32_t)0x00000004U)
#define REG_BIT5_OFFSET    ((uint32_t)0x00000005U)
#define REG_BIT6_OFFSET    ((uint32_t)0x00000006U)
#define REG_BIT7_OFFSET    ((uint32_t)0x00000007U)
#define REG_BIT8_OFFSET    ((uint32_t)0x00000008U)
#define REG_BIT9_OFFSET    ((uint32_t)0x00000009U)
#define REG_BIT10_OFFSET   ((uint32_t)0x0000000AU)
#define REG_BIT11_OFFSET   ((uint32_t)0x0000000BU)
#define REG_BIT12_OFFSET   ((uint32_t)0x0000000CU)
#define REG_BIT13_OFFSET   ((uint32_t)0x0000000DU)
#define REG_BIT14_OFFSET   ((uint32_t)0x0000000EU)
#define REG_BIT15_OFFSET   ((uint32_t)0x0000000FU)
#define REG_BIT16_OFFSET   ((uint32_t)0x00000010U)
#define REG_BIT17_OFFSET   ((uint32_t)0x00000011U)
#define REG_BIT18_OFFSET   ((uint32_t)0x00000012U)
#define REG_BIT19_OFFSET   ((uint32_t)0x00000013U)
#define REG_BIT20_OFFSET   ((uint32_t)0x00000014U)
#define REG_BIT21_OFFSET   ((uint32_t)0x00000015U)
#define REG_BIT22_OFFSET   ((uint32_t)0x00000016U)
#define REG_BIT23_OFFSET   ((uint32_t)0x00000017U)
#define REG_BIT24_OFFSET   ((uint32_t)0x00000018U)
#define REG_BIT25_OFFSET   ((uint32_t)0x00000019U)
#define REG_BIT26_OFFSET   ((uint32_t)0x0000001AU)
#define REG_BIT27_OFFSET   ((uint32_t)0x0000001BU)
#define REG_BIT28_OFFSET   ((uint32_t)0x0000001CU)
#define REG_BIT29_OFFSET   ((uint32_t)0x0000001DU)
#define REG_BIT30_OFFSET   ((uint32_t)0x0000001EU)
#define REG_BIT31_OFFSET   ((uint32_t)0x0000001FU)
#define REG_BIT32_OFFSET   ((uint32_t)0x00000020U)


/** Exported_macro **/

#define SET_BIT(REG, BIT)     ((REG) |= (BIT))

#define CLEAR_BIT(REG, BIT)   ((REG) &= ~(BIT))

#define READ_BIT(REG, BIT)    ((REG) & (BIT))

#define CLEAR_REG(REG)        ((REG) = (0x0U))

#define WRITE_REG(REG, VAL)   ((REG) = (VAL))

#define READ_REG(REG)         ((REG))

#define MODIFY_REG(REG, CLEARMASK, SETMASK)  WRITE_REG((REG), (((READ_REG(REG)) & (~(CLEARMASK))) | (SETMASK)))

/** Peripheral Registers **/
/******************  Bit definition for SCB_CPUID register  *******************/
#define SCB_CPUID_REVISION    ((uint32_t)0x0000000FU) /* Implementation defined revision number */
#define SCB_CPUID_PARTNO      ((uint32_t)0x0000FFF0U) /* Number of processor within family */
#define SCB_CPUID_Constant    ((uint32_t)0x000F0000U) /* Reads as 0x0F */
#define SCB_CPUID_VARIANT     ((uint32_t)0x00F00000U) /* Implementation defined variant number */
#define SCB_CPUID_IMPLEMENTER ((uint32_t)0xFF000000U) /* Implementer code. ARM is 0x41 */

/*******************  Bit definition for SCB_ICSR register  *******************/
#define SCB_ICSR_VECTACTIVE  ((uint32_t)0x000001FFU) /* Active INTSTS number field */
#define SCB_ICSR_RETTOBASE   ((uint32_t)0x00000800U) /* All active exceptions minus the IPSR_current_exception yields the empty set */
#define SCB_ICSR_VECTPENDING ((uint32_t)0x003FF000U) /* Pending INTSTS number field */
#define SCB_ICSR_ISRPENDING  ((uint32_t)0x00400000U) /* Interrupt pending flag */
#define SCB_ICSR_ISRPREEMPT  ((uint32_t)0x00800000U) /* It indicates that a pending interrupt becomes active in the next running cycle */
#define SCB_ICSR_PENDSTCLR   ((uint32_t)0x02000000U) /* Clear pending SysTick bit */
#define SCB_ICSR_PENDSTSET   ((uint32_t)0x04000000U) /* Set pending SysTick bit */
#define SCB_ICSR_PENDSVCLR   ((uint32_t)0x08000000U) /* Clear pending pendSV bit */
#define SCB_ICSR_PENDSVSET   ((uint32_t)0x10000000U) /* Set pending pendSV bit */
#define SCB_ICSR_NMIPENDSET  ((uint32_t)0x80000000U) /* Set pending NMI bit */

/** Bit definition for SCB_AIRCR register **/
#define SCB_AIRCR_VECTCLRACTIVE ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Clear active vector bit */
#define SCB_AIRCR_SYSRESETREQ   ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Requests chip control logic to generate a reset */
#define SCB_AIRCR_ENDIANESS     ((uint32_t)0x00008000U) /* Data endianness bit */
#define SCB_AIRCR_VECTKEYSTAT   ((uint32_t)0xFFFF0000U) /* Reads as 0xFA05 (VECTKEYSTAT) */
#define SCB_AIRCR_VECTKEY       ((uint32_t)0x05FA0000U) /* Register access key */

/** Bit definition for SCB_SCR register **/
#define SCB_SCR_SLEEPONEXIT ((uint8_t)0x02U) /* Sleep on exit bit */
#define SCB_SCR_SLEEPDEEP   ((uint8_t)0x04U) /* Sleep deep bit */
#define SCB_SCR_SEVONPEND   ((uint8_t)0x10U) /* Wake up from WFE */

/** Bit definition for SCB_CCR register **/
#define SCB_CCR_UNALIGN_TRP ((uint16_t)0x0008U) /* Trap for unaligned access */
#define SCB_CCR_STKALIGN    ((uint16_t)0x0200U) /* On exception entry, the SP used prior to the exception is adjusted to be 8-byte aligned */

/** Bit definition for SCB_SHPR register **/
#define SCB_SHPR_PRI_N    ((uint32_t)0x000000FFU) /* Priority of system handler 4,8, and 12. Mem Manage, reserved and Debug Monitor */
#define SCB_SHPR_PRI_N1   ((uint32_t)0x0000FF00U) /* Priority of system handler 5,9, and 13. Bus Fault, reserved and reserved */
#define SCB_SHPR_PRI_N2   ((uint32_t)0x00FF0000U) /* Priority of system handler 6,10, and 14. Usage Fault, reserved and PendSV */
#define SCB_SHPR_PRI_N3   ((uint32_t)0xFF000000U) /* Priority of system handler 7,11, and 15. Reserved, SVCall and SysTick */

/** Bit definition for SCB_SHCSR register **/
#define SCB_SHCSR_SVCALLPENDED   ((uint32_t)0x00008000U) /* SVCall is pended */

/** Bit definition for SCB_DFSR register **/
#define SCB_DFSR_HALTED   ((uint8_t)0x01U) /* Halt request flag */
#define SCB_DFSR_BKPT     ((uint8_t)0x02U) /* BKPT flag */
#define SCB_DFSR_DWTTRAP  ((uint8_t)0x04U) /* Data Watchpoint and Trace (DWT) flag */
#define SCB_DFSR_VCATCH   ((uint8_t)0x08U) /* Vector catch flag */
#define SCB_DFSR_EXTERNAL ((uint8_t)0x10U) /* External debug request flag */

/** Bit definition for SCB_afsr register **/
#define SCB_AFSR_IMPDEF ((uint32_t)0xFFFFFFFFU) /* Implementation defined */



/** Peripheral Registers Bit Definition -- DMA **/

#define DMA_BASE      ((uint32_t)(AHBPERIPH_BASE + (uint32_t)0x0000U))
#define DMA_CH1_BASE  ((uint32_t)(AHBPERIPH_BASE + (uint32_t)0x0008U))
#define DMA_CH2_BASE  ((uint32_t)(AHBPERIPH_BASE + (uint32_t)0x001CU))
#define DMA_CH3_BASE  ((uint32_t)(AHBPERIPH_BASE + (uint32_t)0x0030U))
#define DMA_CH4_BASE  ((uint32_t)(AHBPERIPH_BASE + (uint32_t)0x0044U))
#define DMA_CH5_BASE  ((uint32_t)(AHBPERIPH_BASE + (uint32_t)0x0058U))
#define DMA_CH6_BASE  ((uint32_t)(AHBPERIPH_BASE + (uint32_t)0x006CU))
#define DMA_CH7_BASE  ((uint32_t)(AHBPERIPH_BASE + (uint32_t)0x0080U))


/** DMA_ChannelType **/
typedef struct {
    __IO uint32_t CHCFG;  /* 0x00 */
    __IO uint32_t TXNUM;  /* 0x04 */
    __IO uint32_t PADDR;  /* 0x08 */
    __IO uint32_t MADDR;  /* 0x0C */
    __IO uint32_t CHSEL;  /* 0x10 */
} DMA_ChannelType;

/** DMA **/
typedef struct {
    __I uint32_t INTSTS;                  /* 0x00 */
    __O uint32_t INTCLR;                  /* 0x04 */
    __IO DMA_ChannelType DMA_Channel[7];  /* 0x08 */
} DMA_Module;

#define DMA  ((DMA_Module*)(uintptr_t)DMA_BASE)
#define DMA_CH1  ((DMA_ChannelType*)(uintptr_t)DMA_CH1_BASE)
#define DMA_CH2  ((DMA_ChannelType*)(uintptr_t)DMA_CH2_BASE)
#define DMA_CH3  ((DMA_ChannelType*)(uintptr_t)DMA_CH3_BASE)
#define DMA_CH4  ((DMA_ChannelType*)(uintptr_t)DMA_CH4_BASE)
#define DMA_CH5  ((DMA_ChannelType*)(uintptr_t)DMA_CH5_BASE)
#define DMA_CH6  ((DMA_ChannelType*)(uintptr_t)DMA_CH6_BASE)
#define DMA_CH7  ((DMA_ChannelType*)(uintptr_t)DMA_CH7_BASE)

/** Bit definition for DMA_INTSTS register **/
#define DMA_INTSTS_ERRF7                                        ((uint32_t)0x08000000U) /* Bit27 Transfer error flag for channel 7 */

#define DMA_INTSTS_HTXF7                                        ((uint32_t)0x04000000U) /* Bit26 Half transfer flag for channel 7 */

#define DMA_INTSTS_TXCF7                                        ((uint32_t)0x02000000U) /* Bit25 Transfer complete flag for channel 7 */

#define DMA_INTSTS_GLBF7                                        ((uint32_t)0x01000000U) /* Bit24 Global flag for channel 7 */

#define DMA_INTSTS_ERRF6                                        ((uint32_t)0x00800000U) /* Bit23 Transfer error flag for channel 6 */

#define DMA_INTSTS_HTXF6                                        ((uint32_t)0x00400000U) /* Bit22 Half transfer flag for channel 6 */

#define DMA_INTSTS_TXCF6                                        ((uint32_t)0x00200000U) /* Bit21 Transfer complete flag for channel 6 */

#define DMA_INTSTS_GLBF6                                        ((uint32_t)0x00100000U) /* Bit20 Global flag for channel 6 */

#define DMA_INTSTS_ERRF5                                        ((uint32_t)0x00080000U) /* Bit19 Transfer error flag for channel 5 */

#define DMA_INTSTS_HTXF5                                        ((uint32_t)0x00040000U) /* Bit18 Half transfer flag for channel 5 */

#define DMA_INTSTS_TXCF5                                        ((uint32_t)0x00020000U) /* Bit17 Transfer complete flag for channel 5 */

#define DMA_INTSTS_GLBF5                                        ((uint32_t)0x00010000U) /* Bit16 Global flag for channel 5 */

#define DMA_INTSTS_ERRF4                                        ((uint32_t)0x00008000U) /* Bit15 Transfer error flag for channel 4 */

#define DMA_INTSTS_HTXF4                                        ((uint32_t)0x00004000U) /* Bit14 Half transfer flag for channel 4 */

#define DMA_INTSTS_TXCF4                                        ((uint32_t)0x00002000U) /* Bit13 Transfer complete flag for channel 4 */

#define DMA_INTSTS_GLBF4                                        ((uint32_t)0x00001000U) /* Bit12 Global flag for channel 4 */

#define DMA_INTSTS_ERRF3                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000800U) /* Bit11 Transfer error flag for channel 3 */

#define DMA_INTSTS_HTXF3                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000400U) /* Bit10 Half transfer flag for channel 3 */

#define DMA_INTSTS_TXCF3                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000200U) /* Bit9 Transfer complete flag for channel 3 */

#define DMA_INTSTS_GLBF3                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000100U) /* Bit8 Global flag for channel 3 */

#define DMA_INTSTS_ERRF2                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000080U) /* Bit7 Transfer error flag for channel 2 */

#define DMA_INTSTS_HTXF2                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000040U) /* Bit6 Half transfer flag for channel 2 */

#define DMA_INTSTS_TXCF2                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000020U) /* Bit5 Transfer complete flag for channel 2 */

#define DMA_INTSTS_GLBF2                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit4 Global flag for channel 2 */

#define DMA_INTSTS_ERRF1                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit3 Transfer error flag for channel 1 */

#define DMA_INTSTS_HTXF1                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit2 Half transfer flag for channel 1 */

#define DMA_INTSTS_TXCF1                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit1 Transfer complete flag for channel 1 */

#define DMA_INTSTS_GLBF1                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit0 Global flag for channel 1 */


/** Bit definition for DMA_INTCLR register **/
#define DMA_INTCLR_CERRF7                                       ((uint32_t)0x08000000U) /* Bit27 Clear transfer error flag for channel 7 */

#define DMA_INTCLR_CHTXF7                                       ((uint32_t)0x04000000U) /* Bit26 Clear half transfer flag for channel 7 */

#define DMA_INTCLR_CTXCF7                                       ((uint32_t)0x02000000U) /* Bit25 Clear transfer complete flag for channel 7 */

#define DMA_INTCLR_CGLBF7                                       ((uint32_t)0x01000000U) /* Bit24 Clear global event flag for channel 7 */

#define DMA_INTCLR_CERRF6                                       ((uint32_t)0x00800000U) /* Bit23 Clear transfer error flag for channel 6 */

#define DMA_INTCLR_CHTXF6                                       ((uint32_t)0x00400000U) /* Bit22 Clear half transfer flag for channel 6 */

#define DMA_INTCLR_CTXCF6                                       ((uint32_t)0x00200000U) /* Bit21 Clear transfer complete flag for channel 6 */

#define DMA_INTCLR_CGLBF6                                       ((uint32_t)0x00100000U) /* Bit20 Clear global event flag for channel 6 */

#define DMA_INTCLR_CERRF5                                       ((uint32_t)0x00080000U) /* Bit19 Clear transfer error flag for channel 5 */

#define DMA_INTCLR_CHTXF5                                       ((uint32_t)0x00040000U) /* Bit18 Clear half transfer flag for channel 5 */

#define DMA_INTCLR_CTXCF5                                       ((uint32_t)0x00020000U) /* Bit17 Clear transfer complete flag for channel 5 */

#define DMA_INTCLR_CGLBF5                                       ((uint32_t)0x00010000U) /* Bit16 Clear global event flag for channel 5 */

#define DMA_INTCLR_CERRF4                                       ((uint32_t)0x00008000U) /* Bit15 Clear transfer error flag for channel 4 */

#define DMA_INTCLR_CHTXF4                                       ((uint32_t)0x00004000U) /* Bit14 Clear half transfer flag for channel 4 */

#define DMA_INTCLR_CTXCF4                                       ((uint32_t)0x00002000U) /* Bit13 Clear transfer complete flag for channel 4 */

#define DMA_INTCLR_CGLBF4                                       ((uint32_t)0x00001000U) /* Bit12 Clear global event flag for channel 4 */

#define DMA_INTCLR_CERRF3                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000800U) /* Bit11 Clear transfer error flag for channel 3 */

#define DMA_PADDR7_ADDR_2                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit 2 */
#define DMA_PADDR7_ADDR_3                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit 3 */
#define DMA_PADDR7_ADDR_4                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit 4 */
#define DMA_PADDR7_ADDR_5                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000020U) /* Bit 5 */
#define DMA_PADDR7_ADDR_6                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000040U) /* Bit 6 */
#define DMA_PADDR7_ADDR_7                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000080U) /* Bit 7 */
#define DMA_PADDR7_ADDR_8                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000100U) /* Bit 8 */
#define DMA_PADDR7_ADDR_9                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000200U) /* Bit 9 */
#define DMA_PADDR7_ADDR_10                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000400U) /* Bit 10 */
#define DMA_PADDR7_ADDR_11                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000800U) /* Bit 11 */
#define DMA_PADDR7_ADDR_12                                      ((uint32_t)0x00001000U) /* Bit 12 */
#define DMA_PADDR7_ADDR_13                                      ((uint32_t)0x00002000U) /* Bit 13 */
#define DMA_PADDR7_ADDR_14                                      ((uint32_t)0x00004000U) /* Bit 14 */
#define DMA_PADDR7_ADDR_15                                      ((uint32_t)0x00008000U) /* Bit 15 */
#define DMA_PADDR7_ADDR_16                                      ((uint32_t)0x00010000U) /* Bit 16 */
#define DMA_PADDR7_ADDR_17                                      ((uint32_t)0x00020000U) /* Bit 17 */
#define DMA_PADDR7_ADDR_18                                      ((uint32_t)0x00040000U) /* Bit 18 */
#define DMA_PADDR7_ADDR_19                                      ((uint32_t)0x00080000U) /* Bit 19 */
#define DMA_PADDR7_ADDR_20                                      ((uint32_t)0x00100000U) /* Bit 20 */
#define DMA_PADDR7_ADDR_21                                      ((uint32_t)0x00200000U) /* Bit 21 */
#define DMA_PADDR7_ADDR_22                                      ((uint32_t)0x00400000U) /* Bit 22 */
#define DMA_PADDR7_ADDR_23                                      ((uint32_t)0x00800000U) /* Bit 23 */
#define DMA_PADDR7_ADDR_24                                      ((uint32_t)0x01000000U) /* Bit 24 */
#define DMA_PADDR7_ADDR_25                                      ((uint32_t)0x02000000U) /* Bit 25 */
#define DMA_PADDR7_ADDR_26                                      ((uint32_t)0x04000000U) /* Bit 26 */
#define DMA_PADDR7_ADDR_27                                      ((uint32_t)0x08000000U) /* Bit 27 */
#define DMA_PADDR7_ADDR_28                                      ((uint32_t)0x10000000U) /* Bit 28 */
#define DMA_PADDR7_ADDR_29                                      ((uint32_t)0x20000000U) /* Bit 29 */
#define DMA_PADDR7_ADDR_30                                      ((uint32_t)0x40000000U) /* Bit 30 */
#define DMA_PADDR7_ADDR_31                                      ((uint32_t)0x80000000U) /* Bit 31 */
#define DMA_PADDR7_ADDR_MASK                                    ((uint32_t)0xFFFFFFFFU) /* field mask */


/** Bit definition for DMA_MADDR7 register **/
#define DMA_MADDR7_ADDR                                         ((uint32_t)0xFFFFFFFFU) /* Bit[31:0] Memory address */
#define DMA_MADDR7_ADDR_0                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit 0 */
#define DMA_MADDR7_ADDR_1                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit 1 */
#define DMA_MADDR7_ADDR_2                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit 2 */
#define DMA_MADDR7_ADDR_3                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit 3 */
#define DMA_MADDR7_ADDR_4                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit 4 */
#define DMA_MADDR7_ADDR_5                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000020U) /* Bit 5 */
#define DMA_MADDR7_ADDR_6                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000040U) /* Bit 6 */
#define DMA_MADDR7_ADDR_7                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000080U) /* Bit 7 */
#define DMA_MADDR7_ADDR_8                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000100U) /* Bit 8 */
#define DMA_MADDR7_ADDR_9                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000200U) /* Bit 9 */
#define DMA_MADDR7_ADDR_10                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000400U) /* Bit 10 */
#define DMA_MADDR7_ADDR_11                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000800U) /* Bit 11 */
#define DMA_MADDR7_ADDR_12                                      ((uint32_t)0x00001000U) /* Bit 12 */
#define DMA_MADDR7_ADDR_13                                      ((uint32_t)0x00002000U) /* Bit 13 */
#define DMA_MADDR7_ADDR_14                                      ((uint32_t)0x00004000U) /* Bit 14 */
#define DMA_MADDR7_ADDR_15                                      ((uint32_t)0x00008000U) /* Bit 15 */
#define DMA_MADDR7_ADDR_16                                      ((uint32_t)0x00010000U) /* Bit 16 */
#define DMA_MADDR7_ADDR_17                                      ((uint32_t)0x00020000U) /* Bit 17 */
#define DMA_MADDR7_ADDR_18                                      ((uint32_t)0x00040000U) /* Bit 18 */
#define DMA_MADDR7_ADDR_19                                      ((uint32_t)0x00080000U) /* Bit 19 */
#define DMA_MADDR7_ADDR_20                                      ((uint32_t)0x00100000U) /* Bit 20 */
#define DMA_MADDR7_ADDR_21                                      ((uint32_t)0x00200000U) /* Bit 21 */
#define DMA_MADDR7_ADDR_22                                      ((uint32_t)0x00400000U) /* Bit 22 */
#define DMA_MADDR7_ADDR_23                                      ((uint32_t)0x00800000U) /* Bit 23 */
#define DMA_MADDR7_ADDR_24                                      ((uint32_t)0x01000000U) /* Bit 24 */
#define DMA_MADDR7_ADDR_25                                      ((uint32_t)0x02000000U) /* Bit 25 */
#define DMA_MADDR7_ADDR_26                                      ((uint32_t)0x04000000U) /* Bit 26 */
#define DMA_MADDR7_ADDR_27                                      ((uint32_t)0x08000000U) /* Bit 27 */
#define DMA_MADDR7_ADDR_28                                      ((uint32_t)0x10000000U) /* Bit 28 */
#define DMA_MADDR7_ADDR_29                                      ((uint32_t)0x20000000U) /* Bit 29 */
#define DMA_MADDR7_ADDR_30                                      ((uint32_t)0x40000000U) /* Bit 30 */
#define DMA_MADDR7_ADDR_31                                      ((uint32_t)0x80000000U) /* Bit 31 */
#define DMA_MADDR7_ADDR_MASK                                    ((uint32_t)0xFFFFFFFFU) /* field mask */


/** Bit definition for DMA_CHSEL7 register **/
#define DMA_CHSEL7_CH_SEL                                       ((uint32_t)0x0000003FU) /* Bit[5:0] DMA channel request source selection */
#define DMA_CHSEL7_CH_SEL_0                                     ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit 0 */
#define DMA_CHSEL7_CH_SEL_1                                     ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit 1 */
#define DMA_CHSEL7_CH_SEL_2                                     ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit 2 */
#define DMA_CHSEL7_CH_SEL_3                                     ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit 3 */
#define DMA_CHSEL7_CH_SEL_4                                     ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit 4 */
#define DMA_CHSEL7_CH_SEL_5                                     ((uint32_t)0x00000020U) /* Bit 5 */
#define DMA_CHSEL7_CH_SEL_MASK                                  ((uint32_t)0x0000003FU) /* field mask */



/** Peripheral Registers Bit Definition -- CAN **/

#define CAN_BASE  ((uint32_t)(APB2PERIPH_BASE + (uint32_t)0x4C00U))


/** CAN_TxMailboxType **/
typedef struct {
    __IO uint32_t TMI;   /* 0x00 */
    __IO uint32_t TMDT;  /* 0x04 */
    __IO uint32_t TMDL;  /* 0x08 */
    __IO uint32_t TMDH;  /* 0x0C */
} CAN_TxMailboxType;

/** CAN_RxMailboxType **/
typedef struct {
    __IO uint32_t RMI;   /* 0x00 */
    __IO uint32_t RMDT;  /* 0x04 */
    __IO uint32_t RMDL;  /* 0x08 */
    __IO uint32_t RMDH;  /* 0x0C */
} CAN_RxMailboxType;

/** CAN_FilterBankType **/
typedef struct {
    __IO uint32_t FR1;  /* 0x00 */
    __IO uint32_t FR2;  /* 0x04 */
} CAN_FilterBankType;

/** CAN **/
typedef struct {
    __IO uint32_t MCTRL;                     /* 0x000 */
    __IO uint32_t MSTS;                      /* 0x004 */
    __IO uint32_t TSTS;                      /* 0x008 */
    __IO uint32_t RFF0;                      /* 0x00C */
    __IO uint32_t RFF1;                      /* 0x010 */
    __IO uint32_t INTE;                      /* 0x014 */
    __IO uint32_t ESTS;                      /* 0x018 */
    __IO uint32_t BTIM;                      /* 0x01C */
    uint32_t RESERVED0[88];                  /* 0x020-0x17C */
    __IO CAN_TxMailboxType TxMailbox[3];     /* 0x180 */
    __IO CAN_RxMailboxType RxMailbox[2];     /* 0x1B0 */
    uint32_t RESERVED1[12];                  /* 0x1D0-0x1FC */
    __IO uint32_t FMC;                       /* 0x200 */
    __IO uint32_t FM1;                       /* 0x204 */
    uint32_t RESERVED2;                      /* 0x208 */
    __IO uint32_t FS1;                       /* 0x20C */
    uint32_t RESERVED3;                      /* 0x210 */
    __IO uint32_t FFA1;                      /* 0x214 */
    uint32_t RESERVED4;                      /* 0x218 */
    __IO uint32_t FA1;                       /* 0x21C */
    uint32_t RESERVED5[8];                   /* 0x220-0x23C */
    __IO CAN_FilterBankType FilterBank[14];  /* 0x240 */
} CAN_Module;

#define CAN  ((CAN_Module*)(uintptr_t)CAN_BASE)

/** Bit definition for CAN_MCTRL register **/
#define CAN_MCTRL_DBGF                                          ((uint32_t)0x00010000U) /* Bit16 Debug freeze */

#define CAN_MCTRL_MRST                                          ((uint32_t)0x00008000U) /* Bit15 CAN software master reset */

#define CAN_MCTRL_TTCM                                          ((uint32_t)0x00000080U) /* Bit7 Time triggered communication mode */

#define CAN_MCTRL_ABOM                                          ((uint32_t)0x00000040U) /* Bit6 Automatic bus-off management */

#define CAN_MCTRL_AWKUM                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000020U) /* Bit5 Automatic wake up mode */

#define CAN_MCTRL_NART                                          ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit4 No automatic retransmission */

#define CAN_MCTRL_RFLM                                          ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit3 Receive FIFO locked mode */

#define CAN_MCTRL_TXFP                                          ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit2 Transmit FIFO priority */

#define CAN_MCTRL_SLPRQ                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit1 Sleep mode request */

#define CAN_MCTRL_INIRQ                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit0 Initialization request */


/** Bit definition for CAN_MSTS register **/
#define CAN_MSTS_RXS                                            ((uint32_t)0x00000800U) /* Bit11 CAN Rx signal */

#define CAN_MSTS_LSMP                                           ((uint32_t)0x00000400U) /* Bit10 Last sample point */

#define CAN_MSTS_RXMD                                           ((uint32_t)0x00000200U) /* Bit9 Receive mode */

#define CAN_MSTS_TXMD                                           ((uint32_t)0x00000100U) /* Bit8 Transmit mode */

#define CAN_MSTS_SLAKINT                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit4 Sleep acknowledge interrupt */

#define CAN_MSTS_WKUINT                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit3 Wakeup interrupt */

#define CAN_MSTS_ERRINT                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit2 Error interrupt */

#define CAN_MSTS_SLPAK                                          ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit1 Sleep acknowledge */

#define CAN_MSTS_INIAK                                          ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit0 Initialization acknowledge */


/** Bit definition for CAN_TSTS register **/
#define CAN_TSTS_LOWM2                                          ((uint32_t)0x80000000U) /* Bit31 Lowest priority flag for mailbox 2 */

#define CAN_TSTS_LOWM1                                          ((uint32_t)0x40000000U) /* Bit30 Lowest priority flag for mailbox 1 */

#define CAN_TSTS_LOWM0                                          ((uint32_t)0x20000000U) /* Bit29 Lowest priority flag for mailbox 0 */

#define CAN_TSTS_TMEM2                                          ((uint32_t)0x10000000U) /* Bit28 Transmit mailbox 2 empty */

#define CAN_TSTS_TMEM1                                          ((uint32_t)0x08000000U) /* Bit27 Transmit mailbox 1 empty */

#define CAN_TSTS_TMEM0                                          ((uint32_t)0x04000000U) /* Bit26 Transmit mailbox 0 empty */

#define CAN_TSTS_CODE                                           ((uint32_t)0x03000000U) /* Bit[25:24] Mailbox code */
#define CAN_TSTS_CODE_0                                         ((uint32_t)0x01000000U) /* Bit 0 */
#define CAN_TSTS_CODE_1                                         ((uint32_t)0x02000000U) /* Bit 1 */
#define CAN_TSTS_CODE_MASK                                      ((uint32_t)0x03000000U) /* field mask */

#define CAN_TSTS_ABRQM2                                         ((uint32_t)0x00800000U) /* Bit23 Abort request for mailbox 2 */

#define CAN_TSTS_TERRM2                                         ((uint32_t)0x00080000U) /* Bit19 Transmission error of mailbox 2 */

#define CAN_TSTS_ALSTM2                                         ((uint32_t)0x00040000U) /* Bit18 Arbitration lost for mailbox 2 */

#define CAN_TSTS_TXOKM2                                         ((uint32_t)0x00020000U) /* Bit17 Transmission OK of mailbox 2 */

#define CAN_TSTS_RQCPM2                                         ((uint32_t)0x00010000U) /* Bit16 Request completed mailbox 2 */

#define CAN_TSTS_ABRQM1                                         ((uint32_t)0x00008000U) /* Bit15 Abort request for mailbox 1 */

#define CAN_TSTS_TERRM1                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000800U) /* Bit11 Transmission error of mailbox 1 */

#define CAN_TSTS_ALSTM1                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000400U) /* Bit10 Arbitration lost for mailbox 1 */

#define CAN_TSTS_TXOKM1                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000200U) /* Bit9 Transmission OK of mailbox 1 */

#define CAN_TSTS_RQCPM1                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000100U) /* Bit8 Request completed mailbox 1 */

#define CAN_TSTS_ABRQM0                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000080U) /* Bit7 Abort request for mailbox 0 */

#define CAN_TSTS_TERRM0                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit3 Transmission error of mailbox 0 */

#define CAN_TSTS_ALSTM0                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit2 Arbitration lost for mailbox 0 */

#define CAN_TSTS_TXOKM0                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit1 Transmission OK of mailbox 0 */

#define CAN_TSTS_RQCPM0                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit0 Request completed mailbox 0 */


/** Bit definition for CAN_RFF0 register **/
#define CAN_RFF0_RFFOM0                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000020U) /* Bit5 Release FIFO 0 output mailbox */

#define CAN_RFF0_FFOVR0                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit4 FIFO 0 overrun */

#define CAN_RFF0_FFULL0                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit3 FIFO 0 full */

#define CAN_RFF0_FFMP0                                          ((uint32_t)0x00000003U) /* Bit[1:0] FIFO 0 message pending */
#define CAN_RFF0_FFMP0_0                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit 0 */
#define CAN_RFF0_FFMP0_1                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit 1 */
#define CAN_RFF0_FFMP0_MASK                                     ((uint32_t)0x00000003U) /* field mask */


/** Bit definition for CAN_RFF1 register **/
#define CAN_RFF1_RFFOM1                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000020U) /* Bit5 Release FIFO 1 output mailbox */

#define CAN_RFF1_FFOVR1                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit4 FIFO 1 overrun */

#define CAN_RFF1_FFULL1                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit3 FIFO 1 full */

#define CAN_RFF1_FFMP1                                          ((uint32_t)0x00000003U) /* Bit[1:0] FIFO 1 message pending */
#define CAN_RFF1_FFMP1_0                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit 0 */
#define CAN_RFF1_FFMP1_1                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit 1 */
#define CAN_RFF1_FFMP1_MASK                                     ((uint32_t)0x00000003U) /* field mask */


/** Bit definition for CAN_INTE register **/
#define CAN_INTE_SLKITE                                         ((uint32_t)0x00020000U) /* Bit17 Sleep interrupt enable */

#define CAN_INTE_WKUITE                                         ((uint32_t)0x00010000U) /* Bit16 Wakeup interrupt enable */

#define CAN_INTE_ERRITE                                         ((uint32_t)0x00008000U) /* Bit15 Error interrupt enable */

#define CAN_INTE_LECITE                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000800U) /* Bit11 Last error code interrupt enable */

#define CAN_INTE_BOFITE                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000400U) /* Bit10 Bus-off interrupt enable */

#define CAN_INTE_EPVITE                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000200U) /* Bit9 Error passive interrupt enable */

#define CAN_INTE_EWGITE                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000100U) /* Bit8 Error warning interrupt enable */

#define CAN_INTE_FOVITE1                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000040U) /* Bit6 FIFO 1 overflow interrupt enable */

#define CAN_INTE_FFITE1                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000020U) /* Bit5 FIFO 1 full interrupt enable */

#define CAN_INTE_FMPITE1                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit4 FIFO 1 message pending interrupt enable */

#define CAN_INTE_FOVITE0                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit3 FIFO 0 overflow interrupt enable */

#define CAN_INTE_FFITE0                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit2 FIFO 0 full interrupt enable */

#define CAN_INTE_FMPITE0                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit1 FIFO 0 message pending interrupt enable */

#define CAN_INTE_TMEITE                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit0 Transmit mailbox empty interrupt enable */


/** Bit definition for CAN_ESTS register **/
#define CAN_ESTS_RXEC                                           ((uint32_t)0xFF000000U) /* Bit[31:24] Receive error counter */
#define CAN_ESTS_RXEC_0                                         ((uint32_t)0x01000000U) /* Bit 0 */
#define CAN_ESTS_RXEC_1                                         ((uint32_t)0x02000000U) /* Bit 1 */
#define CAN_ESTS_RXEC_2                                         ((uint32_t)0x04000000U) /* Bit 2 */
#define CAN_ESTS_RXEC_3                                         ((uint32_t)0x08000000U) /* Bit 3 */
#define CAN_ESTS_RXEC_4                                         ((uint32_t)0x10000000U) /* Bit 4 */
#define CAN_ESTS_RXEC_5                                         ((uint32_t)0x20000000U) /* Bit 5 */
#define CAN_ESTS_RXEC_6                                         ((uint32_t)0x40000000U) /* Bit 6 */
#define CAN_ESTS_RXEC_7                                         ((uint32_t)0x80000000U) /* Bit 7 */
#define CAN_ESTS_RXEC_MASK                                      ((uint32_t)0xFF000000U) /* field mask */

#define CAN_ESTS_TXEC                                           ((uint32_t)0x00FF0000U) /* Bit[23:16] Least significant byte of the 9-bit transmit error counter */
#define CAN_ESTS_TXEC_0                                         ((uint32_t)0x00010000U) /* Bit 0 */
#define CAN_ESTS_TXEC_1                                         ((uint32_t)0x00020000U) /* Bit 1 */
#define CAN_ESTS_TXEC_2                                         ((uint32_t)0x00040000U) /* Bit 2 */
#define CAN_ESTS_TXEC_3                                         ((uint32_t)0x00080000U) /* Bit 3 */
#define CAN_ESTS_TXEC_4                                         ((uint32_t)0x00100000U) /* Bit 4 */
#define CAN_ESTS_TXEC_5                                         ((uint32_t)0x00200000U) /* Bit 5 */
#define CAN_ESTS_TXEC_6                                         ((uint32_t)0x00400000U) /* Bit 6 */
#define CAN_ESTS_TXEC_7                                         ((uint32_t)0x00800000U) /* Bit 7 */
#define CAN_ESTS_TXEC_MASK                                      ((uint32_t)0x00FF0000U) /* field mask */

#define CAN_ESTS_LEC                                            ((uint32_t)0x00000070U) /* Bit[6:4] The Last error code */
#define CAN_ESTS_LEC_0                                          ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit 0 */
#define CAN_ESTS_LEC_1                                          ((uint32_t)0x00000020U) /* Bit 1 */
#define CAN_ESTS_LEC_2                                          ((uint32_t)0x00000040U) /* Bit 2 */
#define CAN_ESTS_LEC_MASK                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000070U) /* field mask */

#define CAN_ESTS_BOFFL                                          ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit2 Bus-off flag */

#define CAN_ESTS_EPVFL                                          ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit1 Error passive flag */

#define CAN_ESTS_EWGFL                                          ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit0 Error warning flag */


/** Bit definition for CAN_BTIM register **/
#define CAN_BTIM_SLM                                            ((uint32_t)0x80000000U) /* Bit31 Silent mode */

#define CAN_BTIM_LBM                                            ((uint32_t)0x40000000U) /* Bit30 Loop back mode */

#define CAN_BTIM_RSJW                                           ((uint32_t)0x03000000U) /* Bit[25:24] Resynchronization jump width */
#define CAN_BTIM_RSJW_0                                         ((uint32_t)0x01000000U) /* Bit 0 */
#define CAN_BTIM_RSJW_1                                         ((uint32_t)0x02000000U) /* Bit 1 */
#define CAN_BTIM_RSJW_MASK                                      ((uint32_t)0x03000000U) /* field mask */

#define CAN_BTIM_TBS2                                           ((uint32_t)0x00700000U) /* Bit[22:20] Time segment 2 */
#define CAN_BTIM_TBS2_0                                         ((uint32_t)0x00100000U) /* Bit 0 */
#define CAN_BTIM_TBS2_1                                         ((uint32_t)0x00200000U) /* Bit 1 */
#define CAN_BTIM_TBS2_2                                         ((uint32_t)0x00400000U) /* Bit 2 */
#define CAN_BTIM_TBS2_MASK                                      ((uint32_t)0x00700000U) /* field mask */

#define CAN_BTIM_TBS1                                           ((uint32_t)0x000F0000U) /* Bit[19:16] Time segment 1 */
#define CAN_BTIM_TBS1_0                                         ((uint32_t)0x00010000U) /* Bit 0 */
#define CAN_BTIM_TBS1_1                                         ((uint32_t)0x00020000U) /* Bit 1 */
#define CAN_BTIM_TBS1_2                                         ((uint32_t)0x00040000U) /* Bit 2 */
#define CAN_BTIM_TBS1_3                                         ((uint32_t)0x00080000U) /* Bit 3 */
#define CAN_BTIM_TBS1_MASK                                      ((uint32_t)0x000F0000U) /* field mask */

#define CAN_BTIM_BRTP                                           ((uint32_t)0x000003FFU) /* Bit[9:0] Baud rate prescaler */
#define CAN_BTIM_BRTP_0                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit 0 */
#define CAN_BTIM_BRTP_1                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit 1 */
#define CAN_BTIM_BRTP_2                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit 2 */
#define CAN_BTIM_BRTP_3                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit 3 */
#define CAN_BTIM_BRTP_4                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit 4 */
#define CAN_BTIM_BRTP_5                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000020U) /* Bit 5 */
#define CAN_BTIM_BRTP_6                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000040U) /* Bit 6 */
#define CAN_BTIM_BRTP_7                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000080U) /* Bit 7 */
#define CAN_BTIM_BRTP_8                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000100U) /* Bit 8 */
#define CAN_BTIM_BRTP_9                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000200U) /* Bit 9 */
#define CAN_BTIM_BRTP_MASK                                      ((uint32_t)0x000003FFU) /* field mask */


/** Bit definition for CAN_TMI0 register **/
#define CAN_TMI0_STDID_EXTID                                    ((uint32_t)0xFFE00000U) /* Bit[31:21] Standard identifier or extended identifier */
#define CAN_TMI0_STDID_EXTID_0                                  ((uint32_t)0x00200000U) /* Bit 0 */
#define CAN_TMI0_STDID_EXTID_1                                  ((uint32_t)0x00400000U) /* Bit 1 */
#define CAN_TMI0_STDID_EXTID_2                                  ((uint32_t)0x00800000U) /* Bit 2 */
#define CAN_TMI0_STDID_EXTID_3                                  ((uint32_t)0x01000000U) /* Bit 3 */
#define CAN_TMI0_STDID_EXTID_4                                  ((uint32_t)0x02000000U) /* Bit 4 */
#define CAN_TMI0_STDID_EXTID_5                                  ((uint32_t)0x04000000U) /* Bit 5 */
#define CAN_TMI0_STDID_EXTID_6                                  ((uint32_t)0x08000000U) /* Bit 6 */
#define CAN_TMI0_STDID_EXTID_7                                  ((uint32_t)0x10000000U) /* Bit 7 */
#define CAN_TMI0_STDID_EXTID_8                                  ((uint32_t)0x20000000U) /* Bit 8 */
#define CAN_TMI0_STDID_EXTID_9                                  ((uint32_t)0x40000000U) /* Bit 9 */
#define CAN_TMI0_STDID_EXTID_10                                 ((uint32_t)0x80000000U) /* Bit 10 */

/** Peripheral Registers Bit Definition -- EXTI **/

#define EXTI_BASE                    ((uint32_t)(APB2PERIPH_BASE + (uint32_t)0x0400U))

/** EXTI **/
typedef struct {
    __IO uint32_t IMASK;   /* 0x00 */
    __IO uint32_t EMASK;   /* 0x04 */
    __IO uint32_t RT_CFG;  /* 0x08 */
    __IO uint32_t FT_CFG;  /* 0x0C */
    __IO uint32_t SWIE;    /* 0x10 */
    __IO uint32_t PEND;    /* 0x14 */
} EXTI_Module;

#define EXTI  ((EXTI_Module*)(uintptr_t)EXTI_BASE)

/** Peripheral Registers Bit Definition -- IWDG **/

#define IWDG_BASE  ((uint32_t)(APB1PERIPH_BASE + (uint32_t)0x3000U))


/** IWDG **/
typedef struct {
    __IO uint32_t KEY;     /* 0x00 */
    __IO uint32_t STS;     /* 0x04 */
    __IO uint32_t PREDIV;  /* 0x08 */
    __IO uint32_t RELV;    /* 0x0C */
} IWDG_Module;

#define IWDG  ((IWDG_Module*)(uintptr_t)IWDG_BASE)

/** Bit definition for IWDG_KEY register **/
#define IWDG_KEY_KEYV                                           ((uint32_t)0x0000FFFFU) /* Bit[15:0] Key value register: only certain value will serve particular function */
#define IWDG_KEY_KEYV_0                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit 0 */
#define IWDG_KEY_KEYV_1                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit 1 */
#define IWDG_KEY_KEYV_2                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit 2 */
#define IWDG_KEY_KEYV_3                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit 3 */
#define IWDG_KEY_KEYV_4                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit 4 */
#define IWDG_KEY_KEYV_5                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000020U) /* Bit 5 */
#define IWDG_KEY_KEYV_6                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000040U) /* Bit 6 */
#define IWDG_KEY_KEYV_7                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000080U) /* Bit 7 */
#define IWDG_KEY_KEYV_8                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000100U) /* Bit 8 */
#define IWDG_KEY_KEYV_9                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000200U) /* Bit 9 */
#define IWDG_KEY_KEYV_10                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000400U) /* Bit 10 */
#define IWDG_KEY_KEYV_11                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000800U) /* Bit 11 */
#define IWDG_KEY_KEYV_12                                        ((uint32_t)0x00001000U) /* Bit 12 */
#define IWDG_KEY_KEYV_13                                        ((uint32_t)0x00002000U) /* Bit 13 */
#define IWDG_KEY_KEYV_14                                        ((uint32_t)0x00004000U) /* Bit 14 */
#define IWDG_KEY_KEYV_15                                        ((uint32_t)0x00008000U) /* Bit 15 */
#define IWDG_KEY_KEYV_MASK                                      ((uint32_t)0x0000FFFFU) /* field mask */


/** Bit definition for IWDG_STS register **/
#define IWDG_STS_FRZF                                           ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit2 IWDG freeze flag */

#define IWDG_STS_CRVU                                           ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit1 Watchdog reload value update */

#define IWDG_STS_PVU                                            ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit0 Watchdog prescaler value update */


/** Bit definition for IWDG_PREDIV register **/
#define IWDG_PREDIV_PD                                          ((uint32_t)0x00000007U) /* Bit[2:0] Prescaler factor */
#define IWDG_PREDIV_PD_0                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit 0 */
#define IWDG_PREDIV_PD_1                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit 1 */
#define IWDG_PREDIV_PD_2                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit 2 */
#define IWDG_PREDIV_PD_MASK                                     ((uint32_t)0x00000007U) /* field mask */


/** Bit definition for IWDG_RELV register **/
#define IWDG_RELV_REL                                           ((uint32_t)0x00000FFFU) /* Bit[11:0] Watchdog counter reload value */
#define IWDG_RELV_REL_0                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit 0 */
#define IWDG_RELV_REL_1                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit 1 */
#define IWDG_RELV_REL_2                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit 2 */
#define IWDG_RELV_REL_3                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit 3 */
#define IWDG_RELV_REL_4                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit 4 */
#define IWDG_RELV_REL_5                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000020U) /* Bit 5 */
#define IWDG_RELV_REL_6                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000040U) /* Bit 6 */
#define IWDG_RELV_REL_7                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000080U) /* Bit 7 */
#define IWDG_RELV_REL_8                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000100U) /* Bit 8 */
#define IWDG_RELV_REL_9                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000200U) /* Bit 9 */
#define IWDG_RELV_REL_10                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000400U) /* Bit 10 */
#define IWDG_RELV_REL_11                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000800U) /* Bit 11 */
#define IWDG_RELV_REL_MASK                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000FFFU) /* field mask */



/** Peripheral Registers Bit Definition -- WWDG **/

#define WWDG_BASE  ((uint32_t)(APB1PERIPH_BASE + (uint32_t)0x2C00U))


/** WWDG **/
typedef struct {
    __IO uint32_t CFG;   /* 0x00 */
    __IO uint32_t CTRL;  /* 0x04 */
    __IO uint32_t STS;   /* 0x08 */
} WWDG_Module;

#define WWDG  ((WWDG_Module*)(uintptr_t)WWDG_BASE)

/** Bit definition for WWDG_CFG register **/
#define WWDG_CFG_EWINT                                          ((uint32_t)0x00010000U) /* Bit16 Early wake-up interrupt */

#define WWDG_CFG_TIMERB                                         ((uint32_t)0x0000C000U) /* Bit[15:14] Timer base */
#define WWDG_CFG_TIMERB_0                                       ((uint32_t)0x00004000U) /* Bit 0 */
#define WWDG_CFG_TIMERB_1                                       ((uint32_t)0x00008000U) /* Bit 1 */
#define WWDG_CFG_TIMERB_MASK                                    ((uint32_t)0x0000C000U) /* field mask */

#define WWDG_CFG_W                                              ((uint32_t)0x00003FFFU) /* Bit[13:0] 14-bit window value */
#define WWDG_CFG_W_0                                            ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit 0 */
#define WWDG_CFG_W_1                                            ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit 1 */
#define WWDG_CFG_W_2                                            ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit 2 */
#define WWDG_CFG_W_3                                            ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit 3 */
#define WWDG_CFG_W_4                                            ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit 4 */
#define WWDG_CFG_W_5                                            ((uint32_t)0x00000020U) /* Bit 5 */
#define WWDG_CFG_W_6                                            ((uint32_t)0x00000040U) /* Bit 6 */
#define WWDG_CFG_W_7                                            ((uint32_t)0x00000080U) /* Bit 7 */
#define WWDG_CFG_W_8                                            ((uint32_t)0x00000100U) /* Bit 8 */
#define WWDG_CFG_W_9                                            ((uint32_t)0x00000200U) /* Bit 9 */
#define WWDG_CFG_W_10                                           ((uint32_t)0x00000400U) /* Bit 10 */
#define WWDG_CFG_W_11                                           ((uint32_t)0x00000800U) /* Bit 11 */
#define WWDG_CFG_W_12                                           ((uint32_t)0x00001000U) /* Bit 12 */
#define WWDG_CFG_W_13                                           ((uint32_t)0x00002000U) /* Bit 13 */
#define WWDG_CFG_W_MASK                                         ((uint32_t)0x00003FFFU) /* field mask */


/** Bit definition for WWDG_CTRL register **/
#define WWDG_CTRL_ACTB                                          ((uint32_t)0x00004000U) /* Bit14 Activation bit */

#define WWDG_CTRL_T                                             ((uint32_t)0x00003FFFU) /* Bit[13:0] These bits contain the value of the watchdog counter. */
#define WWDG_CTRL_T_0                                           ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit 0 */
#define WWDG_CTRL_T_1                                           ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit 1 */
#define WWDG_CTRL_T_2                                           ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit 2 */
#define WWDG_CTRL_T_3                                           ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit 3 */
#define WWDG_CTRL_T_4                                           ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit 4 */
#define WWDG_CTRL_T_5                                           ((uint32_t)0x00000020U) /* Bit 5 */
#define WWDG_CTRL_T_6                                           ((uint32_t)0x00000040U) /* Bit 6 */
#define WWDG_CTRL_T_7                                           ((uint32_t)0x00000080U) /* Bit 7 */
#define WWDG_CTRL_T_8                                           ((uint32_t)0x00000100U) /* Bit 8 */
#define WWDG_CTRL_T_9                                           ((uint32_t)0x00000200U) /* Bit 9 */
#define WWDG_CTRL_T_10                                          ((uint32_t)0x00000400U) /* Bit 10 */
#define WWDG_CTRL_T_11                                          ((uint32_t)0x00000800U) /* Bit 11 */
#define WWDG_CTRL_T_12                                          ((uint32_t)0x00001000U) /* Bit 12 */
#define WWDG_CTRL_T_13                                          ((uint32_t)0x00002000U) /* Bit 13 */
#define WWDG_CTRL_T_MASK                                        ((uint32_t)0x00003FFFU) /* field mask */


/** Bit definition for WWDG_STS register **/
#define WWDG_STS_EWINTF                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit0 Early wake-up interrupt flag */



/** Peripheral Registers Bit Definition -- AFIO **/

#define AFIO_BASE  ((uint32_t)(APB2PERIPH_BASE + (uint32_t)0x0000U))


/** AFIO **/
typedef struct {
    __IO uint32_t CFG;          /* 0x00 */
    __IO uint32_t EXTI_CFG1;    /* 0x04 */
    __IO uint32_t EXTI_CFG2;    /* 0x08 */
    uint32_t RESERVED0[4];      /* 0x0C-0x18 */
    __IO uint32_t DIGEFT_CFG1;  /* 0x1C */
    __IO uint32_t DIGEFT_CFG2;  /* 0x20 */
    __IO uint32_t DIGEFT_CFG3;  /* 0x24 */
} AFIO_Module;

#define AFIO  ((AFIO_Module*)(uintptr_t)AFIO_BASE)
/** Bit definition for AFIO_CFG register **/
#define AFIO_CFG_EXTIFLITDIS                                    ((uint32_t)0x00020000U) /* Bit17 EXTI interrupt line analog filter disable */

#define AFIO_CFG_IOFILTCFG                                      ((uint32_t)0x00018000U) /* Bit[16:15] IO filter control */
#define AFIO_CFG_IOFILTCFG_0                                    ((uint32_t)0x00008000U) /* Bit 0 */
#define AFIO_CFG_IOFILTCFG_1                                    ((uint32_t)0x00010000U) /* Bit 1 */
#define AFIO_CFG_IOFILTCFG_MASK                                 ((uint32_t)0x00018000U) /* field mask */

#define AFIO_CFG_ADC_ETRI                                       ((uint32_t)0x00004000U) /* Bit14 ADC injected conversion external trigger remapping */

#define AFIO_CFG_ADC_ETRR                                       ((uint32_t)0x00002000U) /* Bit13 ADC regular conversion external trigger remapping */

#define AFIO_CFG_SPI3_NSS                                       ((uint32_t)0x00001000U) /* Bit12 SPI3 NSS mode selection */

#define AFIO_CFG_SPI1_NSS                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000800U) /* Bit11 SPI1 NSS mode selection */

#define AFIO_CFG_SPI2_NSS                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000400U) /* Bit10 SPI2 NSS mode selection */

#define AFIO_CFG_EXTI_ETRI                                      ((uint32_t)0x000000F0U) /* Bit[7:4] Select interrupt line injected conversion external trigger remapping */
#define AFIO_CFG_EXTI_ETRI_0                                    ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit 0 */
#define AFIO_CFG_EXTI_ETRI_1                                    ((uint32_t)0x00000020U) /* Bit 1 */
#define AFIO_CFG_EXTI_ETRI_2                                    ((uint32_t)0x00000040U) /* Bit 2 */
#define AFIO_CFG_EXTI_ETRI_3                                    ((uint32_t)0x00000080U) /* Bit 3 */
#define AFIO_CFG_EXTI_ETRI_MASK                                 ((uint32_t)0x000000F0U) /* field mask */

#define AFIO_CFG_EXTI_ETRR                                      ((uint32_t)0x0000000FU) /* Bit[3:0] Select interrupt line regular conversion external trigger remapping */
#define AFIO_CFG_EXTI_ETRR_0                                    ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit 0 */
#define AFIO_CFG_EXTI_ETRR_1                                    ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit 1 */

/** Peripheral Registers Bit Definition -- GPIO **/

#define GPIOA_BASE  ((uint32_t)(APB2PERIPH_BASE + (uint32_t)0x0800U))
#define GPIOB_BASE  ((uint32_t)(APB2PERIPH_BASE + (uint32_t)0x0C00U))
#define GPIOC_BASE  ((uint32_t)(APB2PERIPH_BASE + (uint32_t)0x1000U))


/** GPIO **/
typedef struct {
    __IO uint32_t PMODE;   /* 0x00 */
    __IO uint32_t POTYPE;  /* 0x04 */
    uint32_t RESERVED0;    /* 0x08 */
    __IO uint32_t PUPD;    /* 0x0C */
    __IO uint32_t PID;     /* 0x10 */
    __IO uint32_t POD;     /* 0x14 */
    __IO uint32_t PBSC;    /* 0x18 */
    __IO uint32_t PLOCK;   /* 0x1C */
    __IO uint32_t AFL;     /* 0x20 */
    __IO uint32_t AFH;     /* 0x24 */
    __IO uint32_t PBC;     /* 0x28 */
    __IO uint32_t DS;      /* 0x2C */
} GPIO_Module;

#define GPIOA  ((GPIO_Module*)(uintptr_t)GPIOA_BASE)
#define GPIOB  ((GPIO_Module*)(uintptr_t)GPIOB_BASE)
#define GPIOC  ((GPIO_Module*)(uintptr_t)GPIOC_BASE)
/** Bit definition for GPIO_PMODE register **/

#define GPIO_AFH_AFSEL11_2                                      ((uint32_t)0x00004000U) /* Bit 2 */
#define GPIO_AFH_AFSEL11_3                                      ((uint32_t)0x00008000U) /* Bit 3 */
#define GPIO_AFH_AFSEL11_MASK                                   ((uint32_t)0x0000F000U) /* field mask */

#define GPIO_AFH_AFSEL10                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000F00U) /* Bit[11:8] Port GPIOx alternate function configuration bit y */
#define GPIO_AFH_AFSEL10_0                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000100U) /* Bit 0 */
#define GPIO_AFH_AFSEL10_1                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000200U) /* Bit 1 */
#define GPIO_AFH_AFSEL10_2                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000400U) /* Bit 2 */
#define GPIO_AFH_AFSEL10_3                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000800U) /* Bit 3 */
#define GPIO_AFH_AFSEL10_MASK                                   ((uint32_t)0x00000F00U) /* field mask */

#define GPIO_AFH_AFSEL9                                         ((uint32_t)0x000000F0U) /* Bit[7:4] Port GPIOx alternate function configuration bit y */
#define GPIO_AFH_AFSEL9_0                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit 0 */
#define GPIO_AFH_AFSEL9_1                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000020U) /* Bit 1 */
#define GPIO_AFH_AFSEL9_2                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000040U) /* Bit 2 */
#define GPIO_AFH_AFSEL9_3                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000080U) /* Bit 3 */
#define GPIO_AFH_AFSEL9_MASK                                    ((uint32_t)0x000000F0U) /* field mask */

#define GPIO_AFH_AFSEL8                                         ((uint32_t)0x0000000FU) /* Bit[3:0] Port GPIOx alternate function configuration bit y */
#define GPIO_AFH_AFSEL8_0                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit 0 */
#define GPIO_AFH_AFSEL8_1                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit 1 */
#define GPIO_AFH_AFSEL8_2                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit 2 */
#define GPIO_AFH_AFSEL8_3                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit 3 */
#define GPIO_AFH_AFSEL8_MASK                                    ((uint32_t)0x0000000FU) /* field mask */


/** Bit definition for GPIO_PBC register **/
#define GPIO_PBC_PBC15                                          ((uint32_t)0x00008000U) /* Bit15 Clear port GPIOx bit y */

#define GPIO_PBC_PBC14                                          ((uint32_t)0x00004000U) /* Bit14 Clear port GPIOx bit y */

#define GPIO_PBC_PBC13                                          ((uint32_t)0x00002000U) /* Bit13 Clear port GPIOx bit y */

#define GPIO_PBC_PBC12                                          ((uint32_t)0x00001000U) /* Bit12 Clear port GPIOx bit y */

#define GPIO_PBC_PBC11                                          ((uint32_t)0x00000800U) /* Bit11 Clear port GPIOx bit y */

#define GPIO_PBC_PBC10                                          ((uint32_t)0x00000400U) /* Bit10 Clear port GPIOx bit y */

#define GPIO_PBC_PBC9                                           ((uint32_t)0x00000200U) /* Bit9 Clear port GPIOx bit y */

#define GPIO_PBC_PBC8                                           ((uint32_t)0x00000100U) /* Bit8 Clear port GPIOx bit y */

#define GPIO_PBC_PBC7                                           ((uint32_t)0x00000080U) /* Bit7 Clear port GPIOx bit y */

#define GPIO_PBC_PBC0                                           ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit0 Clear port GPIOx bit y */


/** Bit definition for GPIO_DS register **/
#define GPIO_DS_DS15                                            ((uint32_t)0xC0000000U) /* Bit[31:30] Port GPIOx drive strength configuration bits */

#define GPIO_DS_DS15_1                                          ((uint32_t)0x80000000U) /* Bit 1 */
#define GPIO_DS_DS15_MASK                                       ((uint32_t)0xC0000000U) /* field mask */

#define GPIO_DS_DS14                                            ((uint32_t)0x30000000U) /* Bit[29:28] Port GPIOx drive strength configuration bits */

#define GPIO_DS_DS14_1                                          ((uint32_t)0x20000000U) /* Bit 1 */
#define GPIO_DS_DS14_MASK                                       ((uint32_t)0x30000000U) /* field mask */

#define GPIO_DS_DS11_1                                          ((uint32_t)0x00800000U) /* Bit 1 */

#define GPIO_DS_DS10                                            ((uint32_t)0x00300000U) /* Bit[21:20] Port GPIOx drive strength configuration bits */

#define GPIO_DS_DS10_1                                          ((uint32_t)0x00200000U) /* Bit 1 */
#define GPIO_DS_DS10_MASK                                       ((uint32_t)0x00300000U) /* field mask */

#define GPIO_DS_DS9                                             ((uint32_t)0x000C0000U) /* Bit[19:18] Port GPIOx drive strength configuration bits */

/** Peripheral Registers Bit Definition -- RTC **/

#define RTC_BASE  ((uint32_t)(APB1PERIPH_BASE + (uint32_t)0x2800U))


/** RTC **/
typedef struct {
    __IO uint32_t INITSTS;  /* 0x00 */
    __IO uint32_t CTRL;     /* 0x04 */
    __IO uint32_t TSH;      /* 0x08 */
    __IO uint32_t DATE;     /* 0x0C */
    __IO uint32_t WRP;      /* 0x10 */
    __IO uint32_t SCTRL;    /* 0x14 */
    __IO uint32_t SUBS;     /* 0x18 */
    uint32_t RESERVED1;     /* 0x1C */
    __IO uint32_t ALARMA;   /* 0x20 */
    __IO uint32_t PRE;      /* 0x24 */
    __IO uint32_t ALARMB;   /* 0x28 */
    __IO uint32_t WKUPT;    /* 0x2C */
    uint32_t RESERVED0;     /* 0x30 */
    __IO uint32_t ALRMASS;  /* 0x34 */
    __IO uint32_t OPT;      /* 0x38 */
    __IO uint32_t ALRMBSS;  /* 0x3C */
    __IO uint32_t CALIB;    /* 0x40 */
} RTC_Module;

#define RTC  ((RTC_Module*)(uintptr_t)RTC_BASE)

/** Bit definition for RTC_INITSTS register **/
#define RTC_INITSTS_RECPF                                       ((uint32_t)0x00010000U) /* Bit16 Recalibration pending flag */

#define RTC_INITSTS_WTF                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000400U) /* Bit10 Wake up timer flag */

#define RTC_INITSTS_ALBF                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000200U) /* Bit9 Alarm B flag */

#define RTC_INITSTS_ALAF                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000100U) /* Bit8 Alarm A flag */

#define RTC_INITSTS_INITM                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000080U) /* Bit7 Enter Initialization mode */

#define RTC_INITSTS_INITF                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000040U) /* Bit6 Initialization flag */

#define RTC_INITSTS_RSYF                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000020U) /* Bit5 Register synchronization flag */

#define RTC_INITSTS_INITSF                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit4 Initialization status flag */

#define RTC_INITSTS_SHOPF                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit3 Shift operation pending flag */

#define RTC_INITSTS_WTWF                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit2 Wakeup timer write flag */

#define RTC_INITSTS_ALBWF                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit1 Alarm B write flag */

#define RTC_INITSTS_ALAWF                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit0 Alarm A write flag. */


/** Bit definition for RTC_CTRL register **/
#define RTC_CTRL_COEN                                           ((uint32_t)0x00800000U) /* Bit23 Calibration output enable */

#define RTC_CTRL_OUTSEL                                         ((uint32_t)0x00600000U) /* Bit[22:21] Output selection */
#define RTC_CTRL_OUTSEL_0                                       ((uint32_t)0x00200000U) /* Bit 0 */
#define RTC_CTRL_OUTSEL_1                                       ((uint32_t)0x00400000U) /* Bit 1 */
#define RTC_CTRL_OUTSEL_MASK                                    ((uint32_t)0x00600000U) /* field mask */

#define RTC_CTRL_OPOL                                           ((uint32_t)0x00100000U) /* Bit20 Output polarity bit */

#define RTC_CTRL_CALOSEL                                        ((uint32_t)0x00080000U) /* Bit19 Calibration output selection */

#define RTC_CTRL_BAKP                                           ((uint32_t)0x00040000U) /* Bit18 This bit can be written by the user to remember if daylight saving time changes have been applied. */

#define RTC_CTRL_SU1H                                           ((uint32_t)0x00020000U) /* Bit17 Subtract 1 hour (winter time change) */

#define RTC_CTRL_AD1H                                           ((uint32_t)0x00010000U) /* Bit16 Add 1 hour (summer time change) */

#define RTC_CTRL_WTIEN                                          ((uint32_t)0x00004000U) /* Bit14 Wakeup timer interrupt enable */

#define RTC_CTRL_ALBIEN                                         ((uint32_t)0x00002000U) /* Bit13 Alarm B interrupt enable */

#define RTC_CTRL_ALAIEN                                         ((uint32_t)0x00001000U) /* Bit12 Alarm A interrupt enable */

#define RTC_CTRL_WTEN                                           ((uint32_t)0x00000400U) /* Bit10 Wakeup timer enable */

#define RTC_CTRL_ALBEN                                          ((uint32_t)0x00000200U) /* Bit9 Alarm B enable */

#define RTC_CTRL_ALAEN                                          ((uint32_t)0x00000100U) /* Bit8 Alarm A enable */

#define RTC_CTRL_HFMT                                           ((uint32_t)0x00000040U) /* Bit6 Hour format bit */

#define RTC_CTRL_BYPS                                           ((uint32_t)0x00000020U) /* Bit5 Bypass values from the shadow registers */

#define RTC_CTRL_REFCLKEN                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit4 RTC_REFIN reference clock detection bit (50 or 60Hz). */

#define RTC_CTRL_WKUPSEL                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000007U) /* Bit[2:0] Wakeup clock selection */
#define RTC_CTRL_WKUPSEL_0                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit 0 */
#define RTC_CTRL_WKUPSEL_1                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit 1 */
#define RTC_CTRL_WKUPSEL_2                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit 2 */
#define RTC_CTRL_WKUPSEL_MASK                                   ((uint32_t)0x00000007U) /* field mask */


/** Bit definition for RTC_TSH register **/
#define RTC_TSH_APM                                             ((uint32_t)0x00400000U) /* Bit22 AM/PM format. */

#define RTC_TSH_HOT                                             ((uint32_t)0x00300000U) /* Bit[21:20] Describes the hour tens value in BCD format */
#define RTC_TSH_HOT_0                                           ((uint32_t)0x00100000U) /* Bit 0 */
#define RTC_TSH_HOT_1                                           ((uint32_t)0x00200000U) /* Bit 1 */
#define RTC_TSH_HOT_MASK                                        ((uint32_t)0x00300000U) /* field mask */

#define RTC_TSH_HOU                                             ((uint32_t)0x000F0000U) /* Bit[19:16] Describes the hour units value in BCD format */
#define RTC_TSH_HOU_0                                           ((uint32_t)0x00010000U) /* Bit 0 */
#define RTC_TSH_HOU_1                                           ((uint32_t)0x00020000U) /* Bit 1 */
#define RTC_TSH_HOU_2                                           ((uint32_t)0x00040000U) /* Bit 2 */
#define RTC_TSH_HOU_3                                           ((uint32_t)0x00080000U) /* Bit 3 */
#define RTC_TSH_HOU_MASK                                        ((uint32_t)0x000F0000U) /* field mask */

#define RTC_TSH_MIT                                             ((uint32_t)0x00007000U) /* Bit[14:12] Describes the minute tens value in BCD format */
#define RTC_TSH_MIT_0                                           ((uint32_t)0x00001000U) /* Bit 0 */
#define RTC_TSH_MIT_1                                           ((uint32_t)0x00002000U) /* Bit 1 */
#define RTC_TSH_MIT_2                                           ((uint32_t)0x00004000U) /* Bit 2 */
#define RTC_TSH_MIT_MASK                                        ((uint32_t)0x00007000U) /* field mask */

#define RTC_TSH_MIU                                             ((uint32_t)0x00000F00U) /* Bit[11:8] Describes the minute units value in BCD format */
#define RTC_TSH_MIU_0                                           ((uint32_t)0x00000100U) /* Bit 0 */
#define RTC_TSH_MIU_1                                           ((uint32_t)0x00000200U) /* Bit 1 */
#define RTC_TSH_MIU_2                                           ((uint32_t)0x00000400U) /* Bit 2 */
#define RTC_TSH_MIU_3                                           ((uint32_t)0x00000800U) /* Bit 3 */
#define RTC_TSH_MIU_MASK                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000F00U) /* field mask */

#define RTC_TSH_SCT                                             ((uint32_t)0x00000070U) /* Bit[6:4] Describes the second tens value in BCD format */
#define RTC_TSH_SCT_0                                           ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit 0 */
#define RTC_TSH_SCT_1                                           ((uint32_t)0x00000020U) /* Bit 1 */
#define RTC_TSH_SCT_2                                           ((uint32_t)0x00000040U) /* Bit 2 */
#define RTC_TSH_SCT_MASK                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000070U) /* field mask */

#define RTC_TSH_SCU                                             ((uint32_t)0x0000000FU) /* Bit[3:0] Describes the second units value in BCD format */
#define RTC_TSH_SCU_0                                           ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit 0 */
#define RTC_TSH_SCU_1                                           ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit 1 */
#define RTC_TSH_SCU_2                                           ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit 2 */
#define RTC_TSH_SCU_3                                           ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit 3 */
#define RTC_TSH_SCU_MASK                                        ((uint32_t)0x0000000FU) /* field mask */


/** Bit definition for RTC_DATE register **/
#define RTC_DATE_YRT                                            ((uint32_t)0x00F00000U) /* Bit[23:20] Describes the year tens value in BCD format */

#define RTC_DATE_YRU_3                                          ((uint32_t)0x00080000U) /* Bit 3 */

#define RTC_DATE_WDU                                            ((uint32_t)0x0000E000U) /* Bit[15:13] Describes which Week day */

#define RTC_DATE_WDU_MASK                                       ((uint32_t)0x0000E000U) /* field mask */

#define RTC_DATE_MOT                                            ((uint32_t)0x00001000U) /* Bit12 Describes the month tens value in BCD format */

#define RTC_DATE_MOU                                            ((uint32_t)0x00000F00U) /* Bit[11:8] Describes the month units value in BCD format */
#define RTC_DATE_MOU_0                                          ((uint32_t)0x00000100U) /* Bit 0 */
#define RTC_DATE_MOU_1                                          ((uint32_t)0x00000200U) /* Bit 1 */
#define RTC_DATE_MOU_2                                          ((uint32_t)0x00000400U) /* Bit 2 */
#define RTC_DATE_MOU_3                                          ((uint32_t)0x00000800U) /* Bit 3 */
