/******************************************************************************/
/*               Peripheral Registers Bit Definition -- USART                 */
/******************************************************************************/

#define USART1_BASE  ((uint32_t)(APB2PERIPH_BASE + (uint32_t)0x3800U))
#define USART2_BASE  ((uint32_t)(APB1PERIPH_BASE + (uint32_t)0x4400U))


/*** USART ***/
typedef struct {
    __IO uint32_t CTRL1;  //0x00
    __IO uint32_t CTRL2;  //0x04
    __IO uint32_t CTRL3;  //0x08
    __IO uint32_t STS;    //0x0C
    __IO uint32_t DAT;    //0x10
    __IO uint32_t BRCF;   //0x14
    __IO uint32_t GTP;    //0x18
    uint32_t RESERVED0;   //0x1C
    uint32_t RESERVED1;   //0x20
    __IO uint32_t RTO;    //0x24
    __IO uint32_t WKUP;   //0x28
} USART_Module;

#define USART1  ((USART_Module*)(uintptr_t)USART1_BASE)
#define USART2  ((USART_Module*)(uintptr_t)USART2_BASE)

/** Bit definition for USART_CTRL1 register **/
#define USART_CTRL1_UEN                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit0 USART enable */

#define USART_CTRL1_RXEN                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit1 Receiver enable */

#define USART_CTRL1_TXEN                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit2 Transmitter enable */

#define USART_CTRL1_PSEL                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit3 Parity selection */

#define USART_CTRL1_PCEN                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit4 Parity control enable */

#define USART_CTRL1_WL                                          ((uint32_t)0x00000020U) /* Bit5 Word length */

#define USART_CTRL1_RCVWU                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000040U) /* Bit6 Receiver wakeup */

#define USART_CTRL1_WUM                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000080U) /* Bit7 Wake up mode */

#define USART_CTRL1_IDLEIEN                                     ((uint32_t)0x00000100U) /* Bit8 IDLE interrupt enable */

#define USART_CTRL1_RXDNEIEN                                    ((uint32_t)0x00000200U) /* Bit9 RXDNE interrupt enable */

#define USART_CTRL1_TXDEIEN                                     ((uint32_t)0x00000400U) /* Bit10 TXDE interrupt enable */

#define USART_CTRL1_TXCIEN                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000800U) /* Bit11 Transmission complete interrupt enable */

#define USART_CTRL1_PEIEN                                       ((uint32_t)0x00001000U) /* Bit12 PE interrupt enable */

#define USART_CTRL1_SDBRK                                       ((uint32_t)0x00002000U) /* Bit13 Send break */

#define USART_CTRL1_DEM                                         ((uint32_t)0x00004000U) /* Bit14 Driver enable mode */

#define USART_CTRL1_DEP                                         ((uint32_t)0x00008000U) /* Bit15 Driver enable polarity selection */

#define USART_CTRL1_DEDT                                        ((uint32_t)0x001F0000U) /* Bit[20:16] Driver enable deassertion time */
#define USART_CTRL1_DEDT_0                                      ((uint32_t)0x00010000U) /* Bit 0 */
#define USART_CTRL1_DEDT_1                                      ((uint32_t)0x00020000U) /* Bit 1 */
#define USART_CTRL1_DEDT_2                                      ((uint32_t)0x00040000U) /* Bit 2 */
#define USART_CTRL1_DEDT_3                                      ((uint32_t)0x00080000U) /* Bit 3 */
#define USART_CTRL1_DEDT_4                                      ((uint32_t)0x00100000U) /* Bit 4 */
#define USART_CTRL1_DEDT_MASK                                   ((uint32_t)0x001F0000U) /* field mask */

#define USART_CTRL1_DEAT                                        ((uint32_t)0x03E00000U) /* Bit[25:21] Driver enable assertion time */
#define USART_CTRL1_DEAT_0                                      ((uint32_t)0x00200000U) /* Bit 0 */
#define USART_CTRL1_DEAT_1                                      ((uint32_t)0x00400000U) /* Bit 1 */
#define USART_CTRL1_DEAT_2                                      ((uint32_t)0x00800000U) /* Bit 2 */
#define USART_CTRL1_DEAT_3                                      ((uint32_t)0x01000000U) /* Bit 3 */
#define USART_CTRL1_DEAT_4                                      ((uint32_t)0x02000000U) /* Bit 4 */
#define USART_CTRL1_DEAT_MASK                                   ((uint32_t)0x03E00000U) /* field mask */

#define USART_CTRL1_OSPM                                        ((uint32_t)0x04000000U) /* Bit26 Oversampling mode */

#define USART_CTRL1_SWAP                                        ((uint32_t)0x08000000U) /* Bit27 Swap TX/RX pins */

#define USART_CTRL1_TEDEN                                       ((uint32_t)0x20000000U) /* Bit29 First byte transmission delay fixed enable */


/** Bit definition for USART_CTRL2 register **/
#define USART_CTRL2_ADDR                                        ((uint32_t)0x0000000FU) /* Bit[3:0] USART address */
#define USART_CTRL2_ADDR_0                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit 0 */
#define USART_CTRL2_ADDR_1                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit 1 */
#define USART_CTRL2_ADDR_2                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit 2 */
#define USART_CTRL2_ADDR_3                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit 3 */
#define USART_CTRL2_ADDR_MASK                                   ((uint32_t)0x0000000FU) /* field mask */

#define USART_CTRL2_STPB                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000060U) /* Bit[6:5] STOP bits */
#define USART_CTRL2_STPB_0                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000020U) /* Bit 0 */
#define USART_CTRL2_STPB_1                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000040U) /* Bit 1 */
#define USART_CTRL2_STPB_MASK                                   ((uint32_t)0x00000060U) /* field mask */

#define USART_CTRL2_CLKEN                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000100U) /* Bit8 Clock enable */

#define USART_CTRL2_CLKPOL                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000200U) /* Bit9 Clock polarity */

#define USART_CTRL2_CLKPHA                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000400U) /* Bit10 Clock phase */

#define USART_CTRL2_LBCLK                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000800U) /* Bit11 Last bit clock pulse */

#define USART_CTRL2_LINMEN                                      ((uint32_t)0x00001000U) /* Bit12 LIN mode enable */

#define USART_CTRL2_LINBDIEN                                    ((uint32_t)0x00002000U) /* Bit13 LIN break detection interrupt enable */

#define USART_CTRL2_LINBDL                                      ((uint32_t)0x00004000U) /* Bit14 LIN break detection length */

#define USART_CTRL2_RTOEN                                       ((uint32_t)0x00008000U) /* Bit15 Receiver timeout enable */

#define USART_CTRL2_RTOCF                                       ((uint32_t)0x00010000U) /* Bit16 Receiver timeout clear flag */

#define USART_CTRL2_RTOITE                                      ((uint32_t)0x00020000U) /* Bit17 Receiver timeout interrupt enable */


/** Bit definition for USART_CTRL3 register **/
#define USART_CTRL3_CTSEN                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit0 CTS enable */

#define USART_CTRL3_CTSIEN                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit1 CTS interrupt enable */

#define USART_CTRL3_RTSEN                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit2 RTS enable */

#define USART_CTRL3_HDMEN                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit3 Half-duplex mode enable */

#define USART_CTRL3_DMATXEN                                     ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit4 DMA transmitter enable */

#define USART_CTRL3_DMARXEN                                     ((uint32_t)0x00000020U) /* Bit5 DMA receiver enable */

#define USART_CTRL3_ERRIEN                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000040U) /* Bit6 Error interrupt enable */

#define USART_CTRL3_IRDAMEN                                     ((uint32_t)0x00000080U) /* Bit7 IrDA mode enable */

#define USART_CTRL3_IRDALP                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000100U) /* Bit8 IrDA low-power */

#define USART_CTRL3_SCMEN                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000200U) /* Bit9 Smart card mode enable */

#define USART_CTRL3_SCNACK                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000400U) /* Bit10 Smart card NACK enable */


/** Bit definition for USART_STS register **/
#define USART_STS_IDLEF                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000040U) /* Bit6 IDLE line detected */

#define USART_STS_TXDE                                          ((uint32_t)0x00000080U) /* Bit7 Transmit data register empty */

#define USART_STS_TXC                                           ((uint32_t)0x00000100U) /* Bit8 Transmission complete */

#define USART_STS_RXDNE                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000200U) /* Bit9 Read data register not empty */

#define USART_STS_CTSF                                          ((uint32_t)0x00000400U) /* Bit10 CTS flag */

#define USART_STS_LINBDF                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000800U) /* Bit11 LIN break detection flag */

#define USART_STS_PEF                                           ((uint32_t)0x00001000U) /* Bit12 Parity error */

#define USART_STS_OREF                                          ((uint32_t)0x00002000U) /* Bit13 Overrun error */

#define USART_STS_NEF                                           ((uint32_t)0x00004000U) /* Bit14 Noise error flag */

#define USART_STS_FEF                                           ((uint32_t)0x00008000U) /* Bit15 Framing error */

#define USART_STS_RTOF                                          ((uint32_t)0x00010000U) /* Bit16 Receiver timeout */


/** Bit definition for USART_DAT register **/
#define USART_DAT_DATV                                          ((uint32_t)0x000001FFU) /* Bit[8:0] Data value */
#define USART_DAT_DATV_0                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit 0 */
#define USART_DAT_DATV_1                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit 1 */
#define USART_DAT_DATV_2                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit 2 */
#define USART_DAT_DATV_3                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit 3 */
#define USART_DAT_DATV_4                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit 4 */
#define USART_DAT_DATV_5                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000020U) /* Bit 5 */
#define USART_DAT_DATV_6                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000040U) /* Bit 6 */
#define USART_DAT_DATV_7                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000080U) /* Bit 7 */
#define USART_DAT_DATV_8                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000100U) /* Bit 8 */
#define USART_DAT_DATV_MASK                                     ((uint32_t)0x000001FFU) /* field mask */


/** Bit definition for USART_BRCF register **/
#define USART_BRCF_DIV_Decimal                                  ((uint32_t)0x0000000FU) /* Bit[3:0] Fractional part of baud rate divider */
#define USART_BRCF_DIV_Decimal_0                                ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit 0 */
#define USART_BRCF_DIV_Decimal_1                                ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit 1 */
#define USART_BRCF_DIV_Decimal_2                                ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit 2 */
#define USART_BRCF_DIV_Decimal_3                                ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit 3 */
#define USART_BRCF_DIV_Decimal_MASK                             ((uint32_t)0x0000000FU) /* field mask */

#define USART_BRCF_DIV_Integer                                  ((uint32_t)0x0000FFF0U) /* Bit[15:4] Integer part of baud rate divider */
#define USART_BRCF_DIV_Integer_0                                ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit 0 */
#define USART_BRCF_DIV_Integer_1                                ((uint32_t)0x00000020U) /* Bit 1 */
#define USART_BRCF_DIV_Integer_2                                ((uint32_t)0x00000040U) /* Bit 2 */
#define USART_BRCF_DIV_Integer_3                                ((uint32_t)0x00000080U) /* Bit 3 */
#define USART_BRCF_DIV_Integer_4                                ((uint32_t)0x00000100U) /* Bit 4 */
#define USART_BRCF_DIV_Integer_5                                ((uint32_t)0x00000200U) /* Bit 5 */
#define USART_BRCF_DIV_Integer_6                                ((uint32_t)0x00000400U) /* Bit 6 */
#define USART_BRCF_DIV_Integer_7                                ((uint32_t)0x00000800U) /* Bit 7 */
#define USART_BRCF_DIV_Integer_8                                ((uint32_t)0x00001000U) /* Bit 8 */
#define USART_BRCF_DIV_Integer_9                                ((uint32_t)0x00002000U) /* Bit 9 */
#define USART_BRCF_DIV_Integer_10                               ((uint32_t)0x00004000U) /* Bit 10 */
#define USART_BRCF_DIV_Integer_11                               ((uint32_t)0x00008000U) /* Bit 11 */
#define USART_BRCF_DIV_Integer_MASK                             ((uint32_t)0x0000FFF0U) /* field mask */


/** Bit definition for USART_GTP register **/
#define USART_GTP_PSCV                                          ((uint32_t)0x000000FFU) /* Bit[7:0] Prescaler value */
#define USART_GTP_PSCV_0                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit 0 */
#define USART_GTP_PSCV_1                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit 1 */
#define USART_GTP_PSCV_2                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit 2 */
#define USART_GTP_PSCV_3                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit 3 */
#define USART_GTP_PSCV_4                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit 4 */
#define USART_GTP_PSCV_5                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000020U) /* Bit 5 */
#define USART_GTP_PSCV_6                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000040U) /* Bit 6 */
#define USART_GTP_PSCV_7                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000080U) /* Bit 7 */
#define USART_GTP_PSCV_MASK                                     ((uint32_t)0x000000FFU) /* field mask */

#define USART_GTP_GTV                                           ((uint32_t)0x0000FF00U) /* Bit[15:8] Guard time value */
#define USART_GTP_GTV_0                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000100U) /* Bit 0 */
#define USART_GTP_GTV_1                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000200U) /* Bit 1 */
#define USART_GTP_GTV_2                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000400U) /* Bit 2 */
#define USART_GTP_GTV_3                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000800U) /* Bit 3 */
#define USART_GTP_GTV_4                                         ((uint32_t)0x00001000U) /* Bit 4 */
#define USART_GTP_GTV_5                                         ((uint32_t)0x00002000U) /* Bit 5 */
#define USART_GTP_GTV_6                                         ((uint32_t)0x00004000U) /* Bit 6 */
#define USART_GTP_GTV_7                                         ((uint32_t)0x00008000U) /* Bit 7 */
#define USART_GTP_GTV_MASK                                      ((uint32_t)0x0000FF00U) /* field mask */


/** Bit definition for USART_RTO register **/
#define USART_RTO_TIME                                          ((uint32_t)0x0FFFFFFFU) /* Bit[27:0] Receiver timeout value */
#define USART_RTO_TIME_0                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit 0 */
#define USART_RTO_TIME_1                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit 1 */
#define USART_RTO_TIME_2                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit 2 */
#define USART_RTO_TIME_3                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit 3 */
#define USART_RTO_TIME_4                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit 4 */
#define USART_RTO_TIME_5                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000020U) /* Bit 5 */
#define USART_RTO_TIME_6                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000040U) /* Bit 6 */
#define USART_RTO_TIME_7                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000080U) /* Bit 7 */
#define USART_RTO_TIME_8                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000100U) /* Bit 8 */
#define USART_RTO_TIME_9                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000200U) /* Bit 9 */
#define USART_RTO_TIME_10                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000400U) /* Bit 10 */
#define USART_RTO_TIME_11                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000800U) /* Bit 11 */
#define USART_RTO_TIME_12                                       ((uint32_t)0x00001000U) /* Bit 12 */
#define USART_RTO_TIME_13                                       ((uint32_t)0x00002000U) /* Bit 13 */
#define USART_RTO_TIME_14                                       ((uint32_t)0x00004000U) /* Bit 14 */
#define USART_RTO_TIME_15                                       ((uint32_t)0x00008000U) /* Bit 15 */
#define USART_RTO_TIME_16                                       ((uint32_t)0x00010000U) /* Bit 16 */
#define USART_RTO_TIME_17                                       ((uint32_t)0x00020000U) /* Bit 17 */
#define USART_RTO_TIME_18                                       ((uint32_t)0x00040000U) /* Bit 18 */
#define USART_RTO_TIME_19                                       ((uint32_t)0x00080000U) /* Bit 19 */
#define USART_RTO_TIME_20                                       ((uint32_t)0x00100000U) /* Bit 20 */
#define USART_RTO_TIME_21                                       ((uint32_t)0x00200000U) /* Bit 21 */
#define USART_RTO_TIME_22                                       ((uint32_t)0x00400000U) /* Bit 22 */
#define USART_RTO_TIME_23                                       ((uint32_t)0x00800000U) /* Bit 23 */
#define USART_RTO_TIME_24                                       ((uint32_t)0x01000000U) /* Bit 24 */
#define USART_RTO_TIME_25                                       ((uint32_t)0x02000000U) /* Bit 25 */
#define USART_RTO_TIME_26                                       ((uint32_t)0x04000000U) /* Bit 26 */
#define USART_RTO_TIME_27                                       ((uint32_t)0x08000000U) /* Bit 27 */
#define USART_RTO_TIME_MASK                                     ((uint32_t)0x0FFFFFFFU) /* field mask */


/** Bit definition for USART_WKUP register **/
#define USART_WKUP_EN                                           ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit0 Low-power wakeup enable */

#define USART_WKUP_DATCLR                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit1 Clear received wakeup data */



/******************************************************************************/
/*               Peripheral Registers Bit Definition -- LPUART                */
/******************************************************************************/

#define LPUART1_BASE  ((uint32_t)(APB1PERIPH_BASE + (uint32_t)0x4800U))
#define LPUART2_BASE  ((uint32_t)(APB1PERIPH_BASE + (uint32_t)0x4C00U))


/*** LPUART ***/
typedef struct {
    __IO uint32_t STS;     //0x00
    __IO uint32_t INTEN;   //0x04
    __IO uint32_t CTRL;    //0x08
    __IO uint32_t BRCFG1;  //0x0C
    __IO uint32_t DAT;     //0x10
    __IO uint32_t BRCFG2;  //0x14
    __IO uint32_t WUDAT;   //0x18
} LPUART_Module;

#define LPUART1  ((LPUART_Module*)(uintptr_t)LPUART1_BASE)
#define LPUART2  ((LPUART_Module*)(uintptr_t)LPUART2_BASE)

/** Bit definition for LPUART_STS register **/
#define LPUART_STS_NF                                           ((uint32_t)0x00000100U) /* Bit8 Noise detection flag */

#define LPUART_STS_WUF                                          ((uint32_t)0x00000080U) /* Bit7 Low-power mode wakeup flag */

#define LPUART_STS_CTS                                          ((uint32_t)0x00000040U) /* Bit6 CTS signal (hardware flow control) flag */

#define LPUART_STS_FIFO_NE                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000020U) /* Bit5 Receive buffer non-empty flag */

#define LPUART_STS_FIFO_HF                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit4 Receive buffer half-full flag */

#define LPUART_STS_FIFO_FU                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit3 Receive buffer full flag */

#define LPUART_STS_FIFO_OV                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit2 Receive buffer overrun flag */

#define LPUART_STS_TXC                                          ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit1 TX complete flag */

#define LPUART_STS_PEF                                          ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit0 Parity check error flag */


/** Bit definition for LPUART_INTEN register **/
#define LPUART_INTEN_WUFIE                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000040U) /* Bit6 Low-power mode wakeup interrupt enable */

#define LPUART_INTEN_FIFO_NEIE                                  ((uint32_t)0x00000020U) /* Bit5 Receive buffer non-empty interrupt enable */

#define LPUART_INTEN_FIFO_HFIE                                  ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit4 Receive buffer half-full interrupt enable */

#define LPUART_INTEN_FIFO_FUIE                                  ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit3 Receive buffer full interrupt enable */

#define LPUART_INTEN_FIFO_OVIE                                  ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit2 Receive buffer overrun interrupt enable */

#define LPUART_INTEN_TXCIE                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit1 TX complete interrupt enable */

#define LPUART_INTEN_PEIE                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit0 Parity check error interrupt enable */


/** Bit definition for LPUART_CTRL register **/
#define LPUART_CTRL_SMPCNT                                      ((uint32_t)0x00004000U) /* Bit14 Specify sampling method */

#define LPUART_CTRL_WUSEL                                       ((uint32_t)0x00003000U) /* Bit[13:12] Wakeup event selection */
#define LPUART_CTRL_WUSEL_0                                     ((uint32_t)0x00001000U) /* Bit 0 */
#define LPUART_CTRL_WUSEL_1                                     ((uint32_t)0x00002000U) /* Bit 1 */
#define LPUART_CTRL_WUSEL_MASK                                  ((uint32_t)0x00003000U) /* field mask */

#define LPUART_CTRL_RTSEN                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000800U) /* Bit11 RTS hardware flow control enable */

#define LPUART_CTRL_CTSEN                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000400U) /* Bit10 CTS hardware flow control enable */

#define LPUART_CTRL_RTS_THSEL                                   ((uint32_t)0x00000300U) /* Bit[9:8] RTS threshold selection */
#define LPUART_CTRL_RTS_THSEL_0                                 ((uint32_t)0x00000100U) /* Bit 0 */
#define LPUART_CTRL_RTS_THSEL_1                                 ((uint32_t)0x00000200U) /* Bit 1 */
#define LPUART_CTRL_RTS_THSEL_MASK                              ((uint32_t)0x00000300U) /* field mask */

#define LPUART_CTRL_WUSTP                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000080U) /* Bit7 LPUART low-power wakeup enable */

#define LPUART_CTRL_DMA_RXEN                                    ((uint32_t)0x00000040U) /* Bit6 DMA RX request enable */

#define LPUART_CTRL_DMA_TXEN                                    ((uint32_t)0x00000020U) /* Bit5 DMA TX request enable */

#define LPUART_CTRL_LOOKBACK                                    ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit4 Loopback self-test */

#define LPUART_CTRL_PCDIS                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit3 Parity control */

#define LPUART_CTRL_FLUSH                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit2 Clear receive buffer */

#define LPUART_CTRL_TXEN                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit1 TX enable */

#define LPUART_CTRL_PSEL                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit0 Odd parity enable */


/** Bit definition for LPUART_BRCFG1 register **/
#define LPUART_BRCFG1_INTEGER                                   ((uint32_t)0x0000FFFFU) /* Bit[15:0] Baud rate configuration register 1 */
#define LPUART_BRCFG1_INTEGER_0                                 ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit 0 */
#define LPUART_BRCFG1_INTEGER_1                                 ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit 1 */
#define LPUART_BRCFG1_INTEGER_2                                 ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit 2 */
#define LPUART_BRCFG1_INTEGER_3                                 ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit 3 */
#define LPUART_BRCFG1_INTEGER_4                                 ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit 4 */
#define LPUART_BRCFG1_INTEGER_5                                 ((uint32_t)0x00000020U) /* Bit 5 */
#define LPUART_BRCFG1_INTEGER_6                                 ((uint32_t)0x00000040U) /* Bit 6 */
#define LPUART_BRCFG1_INTEGER_7                                 ((uint32_t)0x00000080U) /* Bit 7 */
#define LPUART_BRCFG1_INTEGER_8                                 ((uint32_t)0x00000100U) /* Bit 8 */
#define LPUART_BRCFG1_INTEGER_9                                 ((uint32_t)0x00000200U) /* Bit 9 */
#define LPUART_BRCFG1_INTEGER_10                                ((uint32_t)0x00000400U) /* Bit 10 */
#define LPUART_BRCFG1_INTEGER_11                                ((uint32_t)0x00000800U) /* Bit 11 */
#define LPUART_BRCFG1_INTEGER_12                                ((uint32_t)0x00001000U) /* Bit 12 */
#define LPUART_BRCFG1_INTEGER_13                                ((uint32_t)0x00002000U) /* Bit 13 */
#define LPUART_BRCFG1_INTEGER_14                                ((uint32_t)0x00004000U) /* Bit 14 */
#define LPUART_BRCFG1_INTEGER_15                                ((uint32_t)0x00008000U) /* Bit 15 */
#define LPUART_BRCFG1_INTEGER_MASK                              ((uint32_t)0x0000FFFFU) /* field mask */


/** Bit definition for LPUART_DAT register **/
#define LPUART_DAT_DAT                                          ((uint32_t)0x000000FFU) /* Bit[7:0] Write to the data register when sending, read when receiving */
#define LPUART_DAT_DAT_0                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit 0 */
#define LPUART_DAT_DAT_1                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit 1 */
#define LPUART_DAT_DAT_2                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit 2 */
#define LPUART_DAT_DAT_3                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit 3 */
#define LPUART_DAT_DAT_4                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit 4 */
#define LPUART_DAT_DAT_5                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000020U) /* Bit 5 */
#define LPUART_DAT_DAT_6                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000040U) /* Bit 6 */
#define LPUART_DAT_DAT_7                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000080U) /* Bit 7 */
#define LPUART_DAT_DAT_MASK                                     ((uint32_t)0x000000FFU) /* field mask */


/** Bit definition for LPUART_BRCFG2 register **/
#define LPUART_BRCFG2_DECIMAL                                   ((uint32_t)0x000000FFU) /* Bit[7:0] Baud rate configuration register 2 for baud rate error correction */
#define LPUART_BRCFG2_DECIMAL_0                                 ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit 0 */
#define LPUART_BRCFG2_DECIMAL_1                                 ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit 1 */
#define LPUART_BRCFG2_DECIMAL_2                                 ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit 2 */
#define LPUART_BRCFG2_DECIMAL_3                                 ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit 3 */
#define LPUART_BRCFG2_DECIMAL_4                                 ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit 4 */
#define LPUART_BRCFG2_DECIMAL_5                                 ((uint32_t)0x00000020U) /* Bit 5 */
#define LPUART_BRCFG2_DECIMAL_6                                 ((uint32_t)0x00000040U) /* Bit 6 */
#define LPUART_BRCFG2_DECIMAL_7                                 ((uint32_t)0x00000080U) /* Bit 7 */
#define LPUART_BRCFG2_DECIMAL_MASK                              ((uint32_t)0x000000FFU) /* field mask */


/** Bit definition for LPUART_WUDAT register **/
#define LPUART_WUDAT_WUDAT                                      ((uint32_t)0xFFFFFFFFU) /* Bit[31:0] Wakeup data for checking STOP mode wakeup conditions */
#define LPUART_WUDAT_WUDAT_0                                    ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit 0 */
#define LPUART_WUDAT_WUDAT_1                                    ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit 1 */
#define LPUART_WUDAT_WUDAT_2                                    ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit 2 */
#define LPUART_WUDAT_WUDAT_3                                    ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit 3 */
#define LPUART_WUDAT_WUDAT_4                                    ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit 4 */
#define LPUART_WUDAT_WUDAT_5                                    ((uint32_t)0x00000020U) /* Bit 5 */
#define LPUART_WUDAT_WUDAT_6                                    ((uint32_t)0x00000040U) /* Bit 6 */
#define LPUART_WUDAT_WUDAT_7                                    ((uint32_t)0x00000080U) /* Bit 7 */
#define LPUART_WUDAT_WUDAT_8                                    ((uint32_t)0x00000100U) /* Bit 8 */
#define LPUART_WUDAT_WUDAT_9                                    ((uint32_t)0x00000200U) /* Bit 9 */
#define LPUART_WUDAT_WUDAT_10                                   ((uint32_t)0x00000400U) /* Bit 10 */
#define LPUART_WUDAT_WUDAT_11                                   ((uint32_t)0x00000800U) /* Bit 11 */
#define LPUART_WUDAT_WUDAT_12                                   ((uint32_t)0x00001000U) /* Bit 12 */
#define LPUART_WUDAT_WUDAT_13                                   ((uint32_t)0x00002000U) /* Bit 13 */
#define LPUART_WUDAT_WUDAT_14                                   ((uint32_t)0x00004000U) /* Bit 14 */
#define LPUART_WUDAT_WUDAT_15                                   ((uint32_t)0x00008000U) /* Bit 15 */
#define LPUART_WUDAT_WUDAT_16                                   ((uint32_t)0x00010000U) /* Bit 16 */
#define LPUART_WUDAT_WUDAT_17                                   ((uint32_t)0x00020000U) /* Bit 17 */
#define LPUART_WUDAT_WUDAT_18                                   ((uint32_t)0x00040000U) /* Bit 18 */
#define LPUART_WUDAT_WUDAT_19                                   ((uint32_t)0x00080000U) /* Bit 19 */
#define LPUART_WUDAT_WUDAT_20                                   ((uint32_t)0x00100000U) /* Bit 20 */
#define LPUART_WUDAT_WUDAT_21                                   ((uint32_t)0x00200000U) /* Bit 21 */
#define LPUART_WUDAT_WUDAT_22                                   ((uint32_t)0x00400000U) /* Bit 22 */
#define LPUART_WUDAT_WUDAT_23                                   ((uint32_t)0x00800000U) /* Bit 23 */
#define LPUART_WUDAT_WUDAT_24                                   ((uint32_t)0x01000000U) /* Bit 24 */
#define LPUART_WUDAT_WUDAT_25                                   ((uint32_t)0x02000000U) /* Bit 25 */
#define LPUART_WUDAT_WUDAT_26                                   ((uint32_t)0x04000000U) /* Bit 26 */
#define LPUART_WUDAT_WUDAT_27                                   ((uint32_t)0x08000000U) /* Bit 27 */
#define LPUART_WUDAT_WUDAT_28                                   ((uint32_t)0x10000000U) /* Bit 28 */
#define LPUART_WUDAT_WUDAT_29                                   ((uint32_t)0x20000000U) /* Bit 29 */
#define LPUART_WUDAT_WUDAT_30                                   ((uint32_t)0x40000000U) /* Bit 30 */
#define LPUART_WUDAT_WUDAT_31                                   ((uint32_t)0x80000000U) /* Bit 31 */
#define LPUART_WUDAT_WUDAT_MASK                                 ((uint32_t)0xFFFFFFFFU) /* field mask */


/******************************************************************************/
/*               Peripheral Registers Bit Definition -- PWR                   */
/******************************************************************************/

#define PWR_BASE  ((uint32_t)(APB1PERIPH_BASE + (uint32_t)0x7000U))


/*** PWR ***/
typedef struct {
    __IO uint32_t CTRL1;  //0x00
    __IO uint32_t CTRL2;  //0x04
    __IO uint32_t reserved0;
    __IO uint32_t reserved1;
    __IO uint32_t reserved2;
    __IO uint32_t reserved3;
    __IO uint32_t reserved4;
    __IO uint32_t reserved5;
    __IO uint32_t reserved6;
    __IO uint32_t reserved7;
    __IO uint32_t reserved8;
    __IO uint32_t reserved9;
    __IO uint32_t reserved10;
    __IO uint32_t reserved11;
    __IO uint32_t IWDG_CTRL;//0x38
} PWR_Module;

#define PWR  ((PWR_Module*)(uintptr_t)PWR_BASE)

/** Bit definition for PWR_CTRL1 register **/
#define PWR_CTRL1_BLE_OSC_EN                                    ((uint32_t)0x00000020U) /* Bit5 BLE oscillator enable status, 0: BLE exited Sleep, 1: BLE in Sleep */

#define PWR_CTRL1_PWR_MODE_EN                                   ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit3 Low power mode entry enable */

#define PWR_CTRL1_PWR_MODE                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000007U) /* Bit[2:0] System low power mode selection: 000=Active, 010=Sleep, 100=PD */
#define PWR_CTRL1_PWR_MODE_0                                    ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit 0 */
#define PWR_CTRL1_PWR_MODE_1                                    ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit 1 */
#define PWR_CTRL1_PWR_MODE_2                                    ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit 2 */
#define PWR_CTRL1_PWR_MODE_MASK                                 ((uint32_t)0x00000007U) /* field mask */


/** Bit definition for PWR_CTRL2 register **/
#define PWR_CTRL2_BLE_STATE                                     ((uint32_t)0x0000E000U) /* Bit[15:13] BLE system state: 000=Power On, 001=Active, 100=Sleep */
#define PWR_CTRL2_BLE_STATE_0                                   ((uint32_t)0x00002000U) /* Bit 0 */
#define PWR_CTRL2_BLE_STATE_1                                   ((uint32_t)0x00004000U) /* Bit 1 */
#define PWR_CTRL2_BLE_STATE_2                                   ((uint32_t)0x00008000U) /* Bit 2 */
#define PWR_CTRL2_BLE_STATE_MASK                                ((uint32_t)0x0000E000U) /* field mask */

#define PWR_CTRL2_BLE_WKUP                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000100U) /* Bit8 BLE System Wake-up Signal */

#define PWR_CTRL2_PWR_CLKCTRL                                   ((uint32_t)0x00000080U) /* Bit7 Power Supply Clock Control in Sleep Mode */

#define PWR_CTRL2_GPIO_STA                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit3 GPIO state control in Sleep mode, 0: GPIO hold, 1: GPIO high impedance */

#define PWR_CTRL2_BLE_16KMEM_LP                                 ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit2 BLE 16K RAM retention in Sleep mode, 0: retention, 1: power down */

#define PWR_CTRL2_CORE_32KMEM2_LP                               ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit1 CORE 32K RAM2 retention in Sleep mode, 0: retention, 1: power down */

#define PWR_CTRL2_CORE_32KMEM1_LP                               ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit0 CORE 32K RAM1 retention in Sleep mode, 0: retention, 1: power down */


#define PWR_IWDG_CTRL_SLEEP_FRZ                                 ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit1 Freeze the IWDG counter in Sleep mode */

#define PWR_IWDG_CTRL_IDLE_FRZ                                  ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit0 Freeze the IWDG counter in Idle mode */
/******************************************************************************/
/*               Peripheral Registers Bit Definition -- RCC                   */
/******************************************************************************/

#define RCC_BASE  ((uint32_t)(AHBPERIPH_BASE + (uint32_t)0x1000U))

/*** RCC ***/
typedef struct {
    __IO uint32_t CTRL;         //0x00
    __IO uint32_t CFG;          //0x04
    __IO uint32_t CLKINT;       //0x08
    __IO uint32_t APB2PRST;     //0x0C
    __IO uint32_t APB1PRST;     //0x10
    __IO uint32_t AHBPCLKEN;    //0x14
    __IO uint32_t APB2PCLKEN;   //0x18
    __IO uint32_t APB1PCLKEN;   //0x1C
    __IO uint32_t LSCTRL;       //0x20
    __IO uint32_t CTRLSTS;      //0x24
    __IO uint32_t AHBPRST;      //0x28
    __IO uint32_t CFG2;         //0x2C
    __IO uint32_t OSCFCCR;      //0x30
    __IO uint32_t OSCFCSR;      //0x34
    __IO uint32_t OSCFCLSICNT;  //0x38
    __IO uint32_t OSCFCHSICNT;  //0x3C
    uint32_t RESERVED0;         //0x40
    __IO uint32_t DBGMCU_CR;    //0x44
} RCC_Module;

#define RCC  ((RCC_Module*)(uintptr_t)RCC_BASE)

/** Bit definition for RCC_CTRL register **/
#define RCC_CTRL_HSERDF                                         ((uint32_t)0x00020000U) /* Bit17 External High Speed clock ready flag, set by hardware when HSE is ready */

#define RCC_CTRL_HSEEN                                          ((uint32_t)0x00010000U) /* Bit16 External High Speed clock enable, cleared by hardware in Sleep/PD mode */

#define RCC_CTRL_HSITRIM_7                                      ((uint32_t)0x00008000U) /* Bit15 HSI frequency selection: 0=64MHz */

#define RCC_CTRL_HSITRIM_6_0                                    ((uint32_t)0x00007F00U) /* Bit[14:8] Internal High Speed clock trimming value for HSI RC oscillator calibration */
#define RCC_CTRL_HSITRIM_6_0_0                                  ((uint32_t)0x00000100U) /* Bit 0 */
#define RCC_CTRL_HSITRIM_6_0_1                                  ((uint32_t)0x00000200U) /* Bit 1 */
#define RCC_CTRL_HSITRIM_6_0_2                                  ((uint32_t)0x00000400U) /* Bit 2 */
#define RCC_CTRL_HSITRIM_6_0_3                                  ((uint32_t)0x00000800U) /* Bit 3 */
#define RCC_CTRL_HSITRIM_6_0_4                                  ((uint32_t)0x00001000U) /* Bit 4 */
#define RCC_CTRL_HSITRIM_6_0_5                                  ((uint32_t)0x00002000U) /* Bit 5 */
#define RCC_CTRL_HSITRIM_6_0_6                                  ((uint32_t)0x00004000U) /* Bit 6 */
#define RCC_CTRL_HSITRIM_6_0_MASK                               ((uint32_t)0x00007F00U) /* field mask */

#define RCC_CTRL_HSIRDF                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit1 Internal High Speed clock ready flag, set by hardware when HSI is stable */

#define RCC_CTRL_HSIEN                                          ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit0 Internal High Speed clock enable, cleared by hardware in Sleep/PD mode */


/** Bit definition for RCC_CFG register **/
#define RCC_CFG_MCOPRE                                          ((uint32_t)0x60000000U) /* Bit[30:29] MCU output clock prescaler: 00/01=no divide, 10=divide by 2, 11=divide by 4 */
#define RCC_CFG_MCOPRE_0                                        ((uint32_t)0x20000000U) /* Bit 0 */
#define RCC_CFG_MCOPRE_1                                        ((uint32_t)0x40000000U) /* Bit 1 */
#define RCC_CFG_MCOPRE_MASK                                     ((uint32_t)0x60000000U) /* field mask */

#define RCC_CFG_MCO                                             ((uint32_t)0x1E000000U) /* Bit[28:25] MCU clock output selection: 0000=no clock, 0001=LSI, 0010=LSE, 0011=SYSCLK, 0100=HSI div, 0101=HSE, 0110=HCLK */
#define RCC_CFG_MCO_0                                           ((uint32_t)0x02000000U) /* Bit 0 */
#define RCC_CFG_MCO_1                                           ((uint32_t)0x04000000U) /* Bit 1 */
#define RCC_CFG_MCO_2                                           ((uint32_t)0x08000000U) /* Bit 2 */
#define RCC_CFG_MCO_3                                           ((uint32_t)0x10000000U) /* Bit 3 */
#define RCC_CFG_MCO_MASK                                        ((uint32_t)0x1E000000U) /* field mask */

#define RCC_CFG_GPIOFEN                                         ((uint32_t)0x00800000U) /* Bit23 GPIO filter clock enable */

#define RCC_CFG_GPIOFCLKDIV                                     ((uint32_t)0x007E0000U) /* Bit[22:17] GPIO filter clock division based on HCLK: 000000=1, 000001=2, ..., 111111=64 */
#define RCC_CFG_GPIOFCLKDIV_0                                   ((uint32_t)0x00020000U) /* Bit 0 */
#define RCC_CFG_GPIOFCLKDIV_1                                   ((uint32_t)0x00040000U) /* Bit 1 */
#define RCC_CFG_GPIOFCLKDIV_2                                   ((uint32_t)0x00080000U) /* Bit 2 */
#define RCC_CFG_GPIOFCLKDIV_3                                   ((uint32_t)0x00100000U) /* Bit 3 */
#define RCC_CFG_GPIOFCLKDIV_4                                   ((uint32_t)0x00200000U) /* Bit 4 */
#define RCC_CFG_GPIOFCLKDIV_5                                   ((uint32_t)0x00400000U) /* Bit 5 */
#define RCC_CFG_GPIOFCLKDIV_MASK                                ((uint32_t)0x007E0000U) /* field mask */

#define RCC_CFG_HSISRC                                          ((uint32_t)0x00010000U) /* Bit16 System HSI clock source: 0=HSI/2, 1=HSI no divide */

#define RCC_CFG_APB2PRES                                        ((uint32_t)0x00003800U) /* Bit[13:11] APB2 prescaler: 0xx=no divide, 100=2, 101=4, 110=8, 111=16 */
#define RCC_CFG_APB2PRES_0                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000800U) /* Bit 0 */
#define RCC_CFG_APB2PRES_1                                      ((uint32_t)0x00001000U) /* Bit 1 */
#define RCC_CFG_APB2PRES_2                                      ((uint32_t)0x00002000U) /* Bit 2 */
#define RCC_CFG_APB2PRES_MASK                                   ((uint32_t)0x00003800U) /* field mask */

#define RCC_CFG_APB1PRES                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000700U) /* Bit[10:8] APB1 prescaler: 0xx=no divide, 100=2, 101=4, 110=8, 111=16 */
#define RCC_CFG_APB1PRES_0                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000100U) /* Bit 0 */
#define RCC_CFG_APB1PRES_1                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000200U) /* Bit 1 */
#define RCC_CFG_APB1PRES_2                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000400U) /* Bit 2 */
#define RCC_CFG_APB1PRES_MASK                                   ((uint32_t)0x00000700U) /* field mask */

#define RCC_CFG_AHBPRES                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000030U) /* Bit[5:4] AHB prescaler: 0x=no divide, 10=2, 11=4 */
#define RCC_CFG_AHBPRES_0                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit 0 */
#define RCC_CFG_AHBPRES_1                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000020U) /* Bit 1 */
#define RCC_CFG_AHBPRES_MASK                                    ((uint32_t)0x00000030U) /* field mask */

#define RCC_CFG_SCLKSTS                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit2 System clock switch status: 0=HSI selected as SYSCLK, 1=HSE selected */

#define RCC_CFG_SCLKSW                                          ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit0 System clock switch: 0=HSI selected, 1=HSE selected */


/** Bit definition for RCC_CLKINT register **/
#define RCC_CLKINT_LSECSSICLR                                   ((uint32_t)0x00100000U) /* Bit20 LSE failure interrupt clear, set by software to clear LSECSSIF flag */

#define RCC_CLKINT_HSERDICLR                                    ((uint32_t)0x00080000U) /* Bit19 HSE ready interrupt clear, set by software to clear HSERDIF flag */

#define RCC_CLKINT_HSIRDICLR                                    ((uint32_t)0x00040000U) /* Bit18 HSI ready interrupt clear, set by software to clear HSIRDIF flag */

#define RCC_CLKINT_LSERDICLR                                    ((uint32_t)0x00020000U) /* Bit17 LSE ready interrupt clear, set by software to clear LSERDIF flag */

#define RCC_CLKINT_LSIRDICLR                                    ((uint32_t)0x00010000U) /* Bit16 LSI ready interrupt clear, set by software to clear LSIRDIF flag */

#define RCC_CLKINT_LSECSSIEN                                    ((uint32_t)0x00001000U) /* Bit12 LSE failure interrupt enable */

#define RCC_CLKINT_HSERDIEN                                     ((uint32_t)0x00000800U) /* Bit11 HSE ready interrupt enable */

#define RCC_CLKINT_HSIRDIEN                                     ((uint32_t)0x00000400U) /* Bit10 HSI ready interrupt enable */

#define RCC_CLKINT_LSERDIEN                                     ((uint32_t)0x00000200U) /* Bit9 LSE ready interrupt enable */

#define RCC_CLKINT_LSIRDIEN                                     ((uint32_t)0x00000100U) /* Bit8 LSI ready interrupt enable */

#define RCC_CLKINT_LSECSSIF                                     ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit4 LSE failure detection interrupt flag, set by hardware when LSE clock failure detected */

#define RCC_CLKINT_HSERDIF                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit3 HSE ready interrupt flag, set by hardware when HSE is ready */

#define RCC_CLKINT_HSIRDIF                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit2 HSI ready interrupt flag, set by hardware when HSI is ready */

#define RCC_CLKINT_LSERDIF                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit1 LSE ready interrupt flag, set by hardware when LSE is ready */

#define RCC_CLKINT_LSIRDIF                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit0 LSI ready interrupt flag, set by hardware when LSI is ready */


/** Bit definition for RCC_APB2PRST register **/
#define RCC_APB2PRST_USART1RST                                  ((uint32_t)0x00004000U) /* Bit14 USART1 reset */

#define RCC_APB2PRST_ATIM1RST                                    ((uint32_t)0x00001000U) /* Bit12 ATIM1 timer reset */

#define RCC_APB2PRST_SPI2RST                                    ((uint32_t)0x00000400U) /* Bit10 SPI2 reset */

#define RCC_APB2PRST_SPI1RST                                    ((uint32_t)0x00000200U) /* Bit9 SPI1 reset */

#define RCC_APB2PRST_CANRST                                     ((uint32_t)0x00000100U) /* Bit8 CAN reset */

#define RCC_APB2PRST_IOPCRST                                    ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit4 GPIO port C reset */

#define RCC_APB2PRST_IOPBRST                                    ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit3 GPIO port B reset */

#define RCC_APB2PRST_IOPARST                                    ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit2 GPIO port A reset */

#define RCC_APB2PRST_AFIORST                                    ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit0 Alternate function IO reset */


/** Bit definition for RCC_APB1PRST register **/
#define RCC_APB1PRST_PWRRST                                     ((uint32_t)0x10000000U) /* Bit28 PWR reset */

#define RCC_APB1PRST_SPI3RST                                    ((uint32_t)0x00800000U) /* Bit23 SPI3 reset */

#define RCC_APB1PRST_I2C2RST                                    ((uint32_t)0x00400000U) /* Bit22 I2C2 reset */

#define RCC_APB1PRST_I2C1RST                                    ((uint32_t)0x00200000U) /* Bit21 I2C1 reset */

#define RCC_APB1PRST_USART2RST                                  ((uint32_t)0x00020000U) /* Bit17 USART2 reset */

#define RCC_APB1PRST_WWDGRST                                    ((uint32_t)0x00000800U) /* Bit11 Window watchdog reset */

#define RCC_APB1PRST_BTIM1RST                                    ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit4 BTIM1 timer reset */

#define RCC_APB1PRST_GTIM1RST                                    ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit1 GTIM1 timer reset */


/** Bit definition for RCC_AHBPCLKEN register **/
#define RCC_AHBPCLKEN_IRCTRLEN                                  ((uint32_t)0x00004000U) /* Bit14 IRCTRL clock enable */

#define RCC_AHBPCLKEN_ADCEN                                     ((uint32_t)0x00001000U) /* Bit12 ADC clock enable */

#define RCC_AHBPCLKEN_CRCEN                                     ((uint32_t)0x00000040U) /* Bit6 CRC clock enable */

#define RCC_AHBPCLKEN_SRAMEN                                    ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit2 SRAM clock enable in Idle mode */

#define RCC_AHBPCLKEN_DMAEN                                     ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit0 DMA clock enable */


/** Bit definition for RCC_APB2PCLKEN register **/
#define RCC_APB2PCLKEN_USART1EN                                 ((uint32_t)0x00004000U) /* Bit14 USART1 clock enable */

#define RCC_APB2PCLKEN_ATIM1EN                                   ((uint32_t)0x00001000U) /* Bit12 ATIM1 timer clock enable */

#define RCC_APB2PCLKEN_SPI2EN                                   ((uint32_t)0x00000400U) /* Bit10 SPI2 clock enable */

#define RCC_APB2PCLKEN_SPI1EN                                   ((uint32_t)0x00000200U) /* Bit9 SPI1 clock enable */

#define RCC_APB2PCLKEN_CANEN                                    ((uint32_t)0x00000100U) /* Bit8 CAN clock enable */

#define RCC_APB2PCLKEN_IOPCEN                                   ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit4 GPIO port C clock enable */

#define RCC_APB2PCLKEN_IOPBEN                                   ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit3 GPIO port B clock enable */

#define RCC_APB2PCLKEN_IOPAEN                                   ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit2 GPIO port A clock enable */

#define RCC_APB2PCLKEN_EXTIEN                                   ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit1 EXTI clock enable including exti_pclk and exti_hclk */

#define RCC_APB2PCLKEN_AFIOEN                                   ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit0 Alternate function IO clock enable */


/** Bit definition for RCC_APB1PCLKEN register **/
#define RCC_APB1PCLKEN_PWREN                                    ((uint32_t)0x10000000U) /* Bit28 Power interface clock enable */

#define RCC_APB1PCLKEN_SPI3EN                                   ((uint32_t)0x00800000U) /* Bit23 SPI3 clock enable */

#define RCC_APB1PCLKEN_I2C2EN                                   ((uint32_t)0x00400000U) /* Bit22 I2C2 clock enable */

#define RCC_APB1PCLKEN_I2C1EN                                   ((uint32_t)0x00200000U) /* Bit21 I2C1 clock enable */

#define RCC_APB1PCLKEN_USART2EN                                 ((uint32_t)0x00020000U) /* Bit17 USART2 clock enable */

#define RCC_APB1PCLKEN_IWDGEN                                   ((uint32_t)0x00001000U) /* Bit12 Independent watchdog clock enable */

#define RCC_APB1PCLKEN_WWDGEN                                   ((uint32_t)0x00000800U) /* Bit11 Window watchdog clock enable */

#define RCC_APB1PCLKEN_BTIM1EN                                   ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit4 BTIM1 timer clock enable */

#define RCC_APB1PCLKEN_GTIM1EN                                   ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit1 GTIM1 timer clock enable */


/** Bit definition for RCC_LSCTRL register **/
#define RCC_LSCTRL_LPUART2RST                                   ((uint32_t)0x80000000U) /* Bit31 LPUART2 reset */

#define RCC_LSCTRL_LPUART2EN                                    ((uint32_t)0x40000000U) /* Bit30 LPUART2 clock enable */

#define RCC_LSCTRL_LPUART2SEL                                   ((uint32_t)0x20000000U) /* Bit29 LPUART2 clock source selection: 0=APB1 clock, 1=LSI_LSE_CLK */

#define RCC_LSCTRL_LPUART1RST                                   ((uint32_t)0x10000000U) /* Bit28 LPUART1 reset */

#define RCC_LSCTRL_LPUART1EN                                    ((uint32_t)0x08000000U) /* Bit27 LPUART1 clock enable */

#define RCC_LSCTRL_LPUART1SEL                                   ((uint32_t)0x04000000U) /* Bit26 LPUART1 clock source selection: 0=APB1 clock, 1=LSI_LSE_CLK */

#define RCC_LSCTRL_RTCRST                                       ((uint32_t)0x02000000U) /* Bit25 RTC software reset */

#define RCC_LSCTRL_RTCEN                                        ((uint32_t)0x01000000U) /* Bit24 RTC clock enable */

#define RCC_LSCTRL_LPTIMRST                                     ((uint32_t)0x00800000U) /* Bit23 LPTIM reset */

#define RCC_LSCTRL_LPTIMEN                                      ((uint32_t)0x00400000U) /* Bit22 LPTIM clock enable */

#define RCC_LSCTRL_LPTIMSEL                                     ((uint32_t)0x00200000U) /* Bit21 LPTIM clock source selection: 0=APB1 clock, 1=LSI_LSE_CLK */

#define RCC_LSCTRL_RTCSEL                                       ((uint32_t)0x00100000U) /* Bit20 RTC clock source selection: 0=LSI oscillator, 1=LSE oscillator */

#define RCC_LSCTRL_LSITRIM                                      ((uint32_t)0x0007FF00U) /* Bit[18:8] LSI frequency trimming control bits */
#define RCC_LSCTRL_LSITRIM_0                                    ((uint32_t)0x00000100U) /* Bit 0 */
#define RCC_LSCTRL_LSITRIM_1                                    ((uint32_t)0x00000200U) /* Bit 1 */
#define RCC_LSCTRL_LSITRIM_2                                    ((uint32_t)0x00000400U) /* Bit 2 */
#define RCC_LSCTRL_LSITRIM_3                                    ((uint32_t)0x00000800U) /* Bit 3 */
#define RCC_LSCTRL_LSITRIM_4                                    ((uint32_t)0x00001000U) /* Bit 4 */
#define RCC_LSCTRL_LSITRIM_5                                    ((uint32_t)0x00002000U) /* Bit 5 */
#define RCC_LSCTRL_LSITRIM_6                                    ((uint32_t)0x00004000U) /* Bit 6 */
#define RCC_LSCTRL_LSITRIM_7                                    ((uint32_t)0x00008000U) /* Bit 7 */
#define RCC_LSCTRL_LSITRIM_8                                    ((uint32_t)0x00010000U) /* Bit 8 */
#define RCC_LSCTRL_LSITRIM_9                                    ((uint32_t)0x00020000U) /* Bit 9 */
#define RCC_LSCTRL_LSITRIM_10                                   ((uint32_t)0x00040000U) /* Bit 10 */
#define RCC_LSCTRL_LSITRIM_MASK                                 ((uint32_t)0x0007FF00U) /* field mask */

#define RCC_LSCTRL_LSECSSF                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000080U) /* Bit7 LSE failure detection status, set by hardware when LSE clock failure detected */

#define RCC_LSCTRL_LSECSSEN                                     ((uint32_t)0x00000040U) /* Bit6 LSE failure detection mechanism enable */

#define RCC_LSCTRL_LSXSEL                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000020U) /* Bit5 Low speed clock source selection: 0=LSI oscillator, 1=LSE oscillator */

#define RCC_LSCTRL_LSEBP                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit4 External low speed clock oscillator bypass */

#define RCC_LSCTRL_LSERD                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit3 External low speed clock oscillator ready, set by hardware when LSE is ready */

#define RCC_LSCTRL_LSEEN                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit2 External low speed clock oscillator enable */

#define RCC_LSCTRL_LSIRD                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit1 Internal low speed clock oscillator ready, set by hardware when LSI is ready */

#define RCC_LSCTRL_LSIEN                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit0 Internal low speed clock oscillator enable */


/** Bit definition for RCC_CTRLSTS register **/
#define RCC_CTRLSTS_WWDGRSTF                                    ((uint32_t)0x00000080U) /* Bit7 Window watchdog reset flag, set by hardware on WWDG reset */

#define RCC_CTRLSTS_IWDGRSTF                                    ((uint32_t)0x00000040U) /* Bit6 Independent watchdog reset flag, set by hardware on IWDG reset */

#define RCC_CTRLSTS_SFTRSTF                                     ((uint32_t)0x00000020U) /* Bit5 Software reset flag, set by hardware on software reset */

#define RCC_CTRLSTS_PORRSTF                                     ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit4 POR/PDR reset flag, set by hardware on POR/PDR reset */

#define RCC_CTRLSTS_PINRSTF                                     ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit3 PIN reset flag, set by hardware on RESET pin reset */

#define RCC_CTRLSTS_RMRSTF                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit0 Clear reset flags, software writes 1 then 0 to clear reset flags */


/** Bit definition for RCC_AHBPRST register **/
#define RCC_AHBPRST_BLERST                                      ((uint32_t)0x00008000U) /* Bit15 BLE reset */

#define RCC_AHBPRST_IRCTRLRST                                   ((uint32_t)0x00004000U) /* Bit14 IRCTRL interface reset */

#define RCC_AHBPRST_ADCRST                                      ((uint32_t)0x00001000U) /* Bit12 ADC interface reset */


/** Bit definition for RCC_CFG2 register **/
#define RCC_CFG2_ATIM1CLK                                        ((uint32_t)0x80000000U) /* Bit31 Timer1 clock source: 0=APB2 clock, 1=SYSCLK */

#define RCC_CFG2_GTIM1CLK                                        ((uint32_t)0x40000000U) /* Bit30 Timer3 clock source: 0=APB1 clock, 1=SYSCLK */

#define RCC_CFG2_GTIM1FILTCLK                                    ((uint32_t)0x3E000000U) /* Bit[29:25] GTIM1 filter unit clock division based on GTIM1 working clock */
#define RCC_CFG2_GTIM1FILTCLK_0                                  ((uint32_t)0x02000000U) /* Bit 0 */
#define RCC_CFG2_GTIM1FILTCLK_1                                  ((uint32_t)0x04000000U) /* Bit 1 */
#define RCC_CFG2_GTIM1FILTCLK_2                                  ((uint32_t)0x08000000U) /* Bit 2 */
#define RCC_CFG2_GTIM1FILTCLK_3                                  ((uint32_t)0x10000000U) /* Bit 3 */
#define RCC_CFG2_GTIM1FILTCLK_4                                  ((uint32_t)0x20000000U) /* Bit 4 */
#define RCC_CFG2_GTIM1FILTCLK_MASK                               ((uint32_t)0x3E000000U) /* field mask */

#define RCC_CFG2_ATIM1FILTCLK                                    ((uint32_t)0x01F00000U) /* Bit[24:20] ATIM1 filter unit clock division based on ATIM1 working clock */
#define RCC_CFG2_ATIM1FILTCLK_0                                  ((uint32_t)0x00100000U) /* Bit 0 */
#define RCC_CFG2_ATIM1FILTCLK_1                                  ((uint32_t)0x00200000U) /* Bit 1 */
#define RCC_CFG2_ATIM1FILTCLK_2                                  ((uint32_t)0x00400000U) /* Bit 2 */
#define RCC_CFG2_ATIM1FILTCLK_3                                  ((uint32_t)0x00800000U) /* Bit 3 */
#define RCC_CFG2_ATIM1FILTCLK_4                                  ((uint32_t)0x01000000U) /* Bit 4 */
#define RCC_CFG2_ATIM1FILTCLK_MASK                               ((uint32_t)0x01F00000U) /* field mask */

#define RCC_CFG2_CANCLKPRES                                     ((uint32_t)0x000F0000U) /* Bit[19:16] CAN asynchronous clock prescaler */
#define RCC_CFG2_CANCLKPRES_0                                   ((uint32_t)0x00010000U) /* Bit 0 */
#define RCC_CFG2_CANCLKPRES_1                                   ((uint32_t)0x00020000U) /* Bit 1 */
#define RCC_CFG2_CANCLKPRES_2                                   ((uint32_t)0x00040000U) /* Bit 2 */
#define RCC_CFG2_CANCLKPRES_3                                   ((uint32_t)0x00080000U) /* Bit 3 */
#define RCC_CFG2_CANCLKPRES_MASK                                ((uint32_t)0x000F0000U) /* field mask */

#define RCC_CFG2_ADC1MPRE                                       ((uint32_t)0x0000F000U) /* Bit[15:12] ADC 1M clock prescaler */
#define RCC_CFG2_ADC1MPRE_0                                     ((uint32_t)0x00001000U) /* Bit 0 */
#define RCC_CFG2_ADC1MPRE_1                                     ((uint32_t)0x00002000U) /* Bit 1 */
#define RCC_CFG2_ADC1MPRE_2                                     ((uint32_t)0x00004000U) /* Bit 2 */
#define RCC_CFG2_ADC1MPRE_3                                     ((uint32_t)0x00008000U) /* Bit 3 */
#define RCC_CFG2_ADC1MPRE_MASK                                  ((uint32_t)0x0000F000U) /* field mask */

#define RCC_CFG2_ADCACLKPRES                                    ((uint32_t)0x00000F00U) /* Bit[11:8] ADC asynchronous clock prescaler */
#define RCC_CFG2_ADCACLKPRES_0                                  ((uint32_t)0x00000100U) /* Bit 0 */
#define RCC_CFG2_ADCACLKPRES_1                                  ((uint32_t)0x00000200U) /* Bit 1 */
#define RCC_CFG2_ADCACLKPRES_2                                  ((uint32_t)0x00000400U) /* Bit 2 */
#define RCC_CFG2_ADCACLKPRES_3                                  ((uint32_t)0x00000800U) /* Bit 3 */
#define RCC_CFG2_ADCACLKPRES_MASK                               ((uint32_t)0x00000F00U) /* field mask */

#define RCC_CFG2_CANSEL                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit4 CAN clock source selection: 0=HSE async clock, 1=APB2 bus clock */

#define RCC_CFG2_ADCSEL                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit3 ADC clock source selection: 0=HSE clock, 1=HSI/2 clock */


/** Bit definition for RCC_OSCFCCR register **/
#define RCC_OSCFCCR_LSI_TRIM_CFG                                ((uint32_t)0x00007F00U) /* Bit[14:8] LSI trim count configuration, 0~127 LSI cycles configurable */
#define RCC_OSCFCCR_LSI_TRIM_CFG_0                              ((uint32_t)0x00000100U) /* Bit 0 */
#define RCC_OSCFCCR_LSI_TRIM_CFG_1                              ((uint32_t)0x00000200U) /* Bit 1 */
#define RCC_OSCFCCR_LSI_TRIM_CFG_2                              ((uint32_t)0x00000400U) /* Bit 2 */
#define RCC_OSCFCCR_LSI_TRIM_CFG_3                              ((uint32_t)0x00000800U) /* Bit 3 */
#define RCC_OSCFCCR_LSI_TRIM_CFG_4                              ((uint32_t)0x00001000U) /* Bit 4 */
#define RCC_OSCFCCR_LSI_TRIM_CFG_5                              ((uint32_t)0x00002000U) /* Bit 5 */
#define RCC_OSCFCCR_LSI_TRIM_CFG_6                              ((uint32_t)0x00004000U) /* Bit 6 */
#define RCC_OSCFCCR_LSI_TRIM_CFG_MASK                           ((uint32_t)0x00007F00U) /* field mask */

#define RCC_OSCFCCR_HSICALEN                                    ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit1 Start HSI calibration, returns 0 on read */

#define RCC_OSCFCCR_LSICALEN                                    ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit0 Start LSI calibration, returns 0 on read */


/** Bit definition for RCC_OSCFCSR register **/
#define RCC_OSCFCSR_HSICNTDONE                                  ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit1 HSI count done flag, 1024 HSE cycles after HSICALEN set */

#define RCC_OSCFCSR_LSICNTDONE                                  ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit0 LSI count done flag, LSI_TRIM_CFG LSI cycles after LSICALEN set */


/** Bit definition for RCC_OSCFCLSICNT register **/
#define RCC_OSCFCLSICNT_LSICNT                                  ((uint32_t)0x0003FFFFU) /* Bit[17:0] LSI clock calibration count result */
#define RCC_OSCFCLSICNT_LSICNT_0                                ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit 0 */
#define RCC_OSCFCLSICNT_LSICNT_1                                ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit 1 */
#define RCC_OSCFCLSICNT_LSICNT_2                                ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit 2 */
#define RCC_OSCFCLSICNT_LSICNT_3                                ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit 3 */
#define RCC_OSCFCLSICNT_LSICNT_4                                ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit 4 */
#define RCC_OSCFCLSICNT_LSICNT_5                                ((uint32_t)0x00000020U) /* Bit 5 */
#define RCC_OSCFCLSICNT_LSICNT_6                                ((uint32_t)0x00000040U) /* Bit 6 */
#define RCC_OSCFCLSICNT_LSICNT_7                                ((uint32_t)0x00000080U) /* Bit 7 */
#define RCC_OSCFCLSICNT_LSICNT_8                                ((uint32_t)0x00000100U) /* Bit 8 */
#define RCC_OSCFCLSICNT_LSICNT_9                                ((uint32_t)0x00000200U) /* Bit 9 */
#define RCC_OSCFCLSICNT_LSICNT_10                               ((uint32_t)0x00000400U) /* Bit 10 */
#define RCC_OSCFCLSICNT_LSICNT_11                               ((uint32_t)0x00000800U) /* Bit 11 */
#define RCC_OSCFCLSICNT_LSICNT_12                               ((uint32_t)0x00001000U) /* Bit 12 */
#define RCC_OSCFCLSICNT_LSICNT_13                               ((uint32_t)0x00002000U) /* Bit 13 */
#define RCC_OSCFCLSICNT_LSICNT_14                               ((uint32_t)0x00004000U) /* Bit 14 */
#define RCC_OSCFCLSICNT_LSICNT_15                               ((uint32_t)0x00008000U) /* Bit 15 */
#define RCC_OSCFCLSICNT_LSICNT_16                               ((uint32_t)0x00010000U) /* Bit 16 */
#define RCC_OSCFCLSICNT_LSICNT_17                               ((uint32_t)0x00020000U) /* Bit 17 */
#define RCC_OSCFCLSICNT_LSICNT_MASK                             ((uint32_t)0x0003FFFFU) /* field mask */


/** Bit definition for RCC_OSCFCHSICNT register **/
#define RCC_OSCFCHSICNT_HSICNT                                  ((uint32_t)0x00000FFFU) /* Bit[11:0] HSI clock calibration count result */
#define RCC_OSCFCHSICNT_HSICNT_0                                ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit 0 */
#define RCC_OSCFCHSICNT_HSICNT_1                                ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit 1 */
#define RCC_OSCFCHSICNT_HSICNT_2                                ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit 2 */
#define RCC_OSCFCHSICNT_HSICNT_3                                ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit 3 */
#define RCC_OSCFCHSICNT_HSICNT_4                                ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit 4 */
#define RCC_OSCFCHSICNT_HSICNT_5                                ((uint32_t)0x00000020U) /* Bit 5 */
#define RCC_OSCFCHSICNT_HSICNT_6                                ((uint32_t)0x00000040U) /* Bit 6 */
#define RCC_OSCFCHSICNT_HSICNT_7                                ((uint32_t)0x00000080U) /* Bit 7 */
#define RCC_OSCFCHSICNT_HSICNT_8                                ((uint32_t)0x00000100U) /* Bit 8 */
#define RCC_OSCFCHSICNT_HSICNT_9                                ((uint32_t)0x00000200U) /* Bit 9 */
#define RCC_OSCFCHSICNT_HSICNT_10                               ((uint32_t)0x00000400U) /* Bit 10 */
#define RCC_OSCFCHSICNT_HSICNT_11                               ((uint32_t)0x00000800U) /* Bit 11 */
#define RCC_OSCFCHSICNT_HSICNT_MASK                             ((uint32_t)0x00000FFFU) /* field mask */


/** Bit definition for RCC_DBGMCU_CR register **/
#define RCC_DBGMCU_CR_CAN_STOP                                  ((uint32_t)0x00000100U) /* Bit8 Debug CAN stop when core enters debug state */

#define RCC_DBGMCU_CR_LPTIM_STOP                                ((uint32_t)0x00000080U) /* Bit7 Debug LPTIM stop when core enters debug state */

#define RCC_DBGMCU_CR_I2C2_TIMEOUT                              ((uint32_t)0x00000040U) /* Bit6 Freeze SMBUS timeout when core stopped */

#define RCC_DBGMCU_CR_I2C1_TIMEOUT                              ((uint32_t)0x00000020U) /* Bit5 Freeze SMBUS timeout when core stopped */

#define RCC_DBGMCU_CR_BTIM1_STOP                                 ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit4 Debug BTIM1 stop when core enters debug state */

#define RCC_DBGMCU_CR_GTIM1_STOP                                 ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit3 Debug GTIM1 stop when core enters debug state */

#define RCC_DBGMCU_CR_ATIM1_STOP                                 ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit2 Debug ATIM1 stop when core enters debug state */

#define RCC_DBGMCU_CR_WWDG_STOP                                 ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit1 Debug WWDG stop when core enters debug state */

#define RCC_DBGMCU_CR_IWDG_STOP                                 ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit0 Debug IWDG stop when core enters debug state */


/******************************************************************************/
/*               Peripheral Registers Bit Definition -- IRC                   */
/******************************************************************************/

#define IRC_BASE                     ((uint32_t)(AHBPERIPH_BASE + (uint32_t)0x1C00U))

/*** IRC ***/
typedef struct {
    __IO uint32_t FREQ_CARRIER_ON;  //0x00
    __IO uint32_t FREQ_CARRIER_OFF; //0x04
    __IO uint32_t LOGIC_ONE_TIME;   //0x08
    __IO uint32_t LOGIC_ZERO_TIME;  //0x0C
    __IO uint32_t CTRL;             //0x10
    __IO uint32_t STATUS;           //0x14
    __IO uint32_t REPEAT_TIME;      //0x18
    __IO uint32_t CODE_FIFO;        //0x1C
    __IO uint32_t REPEAT_FIFO;      //0x20
} IRC_Module;

#define IRC  ((IRC_Module*)(uintptr_t)IRC_BASE)

/** Bit definition for IRC_FREQ_CARR_ON register **/
#define IRC_FREQ_CARR_ON_FREQ_CARRIER_ON                        ((uint32_t)0x000007FFU) /* Bit[10:0] Define the high duration of carrier clock (the number of AHB bus clock cycles is not allowed to be configured as 0). */
#define IRC_FREQ_CARR_ON_FREQ_CARRIER_ON_0                      ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit 0 */
#define IRC_FREQ_CARR_ON_FREQ_CARRIER_ON_1                      ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit 1 */
#define IRC_FREQ_CARR_ON_FREQ_CARRIER_ON_2                      ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit 2 */
#define IRC_FREQ_CARR_ON_FREQ_CARRIER_ON_3                      ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit 3 */
#define IRC_FREQ_CARR_ON_FREQ_CARRIER_ON_4                      ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit 4 */
#define IRC_FREQ_CARR_ON_FREQ_CARRIER_ON_5                      ((uint32_t)0x00000020U) /* Bit 5 */
#define IRC_FREQ_CARR_ON_FREQ_CARRIER_ON_6                      ((uint32_t)0x00000040U) /* Bit 6 */
#define IRC_FREQ_CARR_ON_FREQ_CARRIER_ON_7                      ((uint32_t)0x00000080U) /* Bit 7 */
#define IRC_FREQ_CARR_ON_FREQ_CARRIER_ON_8                      ((uint32_t)0x00000100U) /* Bit 8 */
#define IRC_FREQ_CARR_ON_FREQ_CARRIER_ON_9                      ((uint32_t)0x00000200U) /* Bit 9 */
#define IRC_FREQ_CARR_ON_FREQ_CARRIER_ON_10                     ((uint32_t)0x00000400U) /* Bit 10 */
#define IRC_FREQ_CARR_ON_FREQ_CARRIER_ON_MASK                   ((uint32_t)0x000007FFU) /* field mask */


/** Bit definition for IRC_FREQ_CARR_OFF register **/
#define IRC_FREQ_CARR_OFF_FREQ_CARR_OFF                         ((uint32_t)0x000007FFU) /* Bit[10:0] Define the low duration of carrier clock (the number of AHB bus clock cycles is not allowed to be configured as 0). */
#define IRC_FREQ_CARR_OFF_FREQ_CARR_OFF_0                       ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit 0 */
#define IRC_FREQ_CARR_OFF_FREQ_CARR_OFF_1                       ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit 1 */
#define IRC_FREQ_CARR_OFF_FREQ_CARR_OFF_2                       ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit 2 */
#define IRC_FREQ_CARR_OFF_FREQ_CARR_OFF_3                       ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit 3 */
#define IRC_FREQ_CARR_OFF_FREQ_CARR_OFF_4                       ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit 4 */
#define IRC_FREQ_CARR_OFF_FREQ_CARR_OFF_5                       ((uint32_t)0x00000020U) /* Bit 5 */
#define IRC_FREQ_CARR_OFF_FREQ_CARR_OFF_6                       ((uint32_t)0x00000040U) /* Bit 6 */
#define IRC_FREQ_CARR_OFF_FREQ_CARR_OFF_7                       ((uint32_t)0x00000080U) /* Bit 7 */
#define IRC_FREQ_CARR_OFF_FREQ_CARR_OFF_8                       ((uint32_t)0x00000100U) /* Bit 8 */
#define IRC_FREQ_CARR_OFF_FREQ_CARR_OFF_9                       ((uint32_t)0x00000200U) /* Bit 9 */
#define IRC_FREQ_CARR_OFF_FREQ_CARR_OFF_10                      ((uint32_t)0x00000400U) /* Bit 10 */
#define IRC_FREQ_CARR_OFF_FREQ_CARR_OFF_MASK                    ((uint32_t)0x000007FFU) /* field mask */


/** Bit definition for IRC_LOGIC_ONE_TIME register **/
#define IRC_LOGIC_ONE_TIME_LOGIC_ONE_MARK                       ((uint32_t)0x0000FF00U) /* Bit[15:8] Logic 1 MARK time (carrier clock cycle) */
#define IRC_LOGIC_ONE_TIME_LOGIC_ONE_MARK_0                     ((uint32_t)0x00000100U) /* Bit 0 */
#define IRC_LOGIC_ONE_TIME_LOGIC_ONE_MARK_1                     ((uint32_t)0x00000200U) /* Bit 1 */
#define IRC_LOGIC_ONE_TIME_LOGIC_ONE_MARK_2                     ((uint32_t)0x00000400U) /* Bit 2 */
#define IRC_LOGIC_ONE_TIME_LOGIC_ONE_MARK_3                     ((uint32_t)0x00000800U) /* Bit 3 */
#define IRC_LOGIC_ONE_TIME_LOGIC_ONE_MARK_4                     ((uint32_t)0x00001000U) /* Bit 4 */
#define IRC_LOGIC_ONE_TIME_LOGIC_ONE_MARK_5                     ((uint32_t)0x00002000U) /* Bit 5 */
#define IRC_LOGIC_ONE_TIME_LOGIC_ONE_MARK_6                     ((uint32_t)0x00004000U) /* Bit 6 */
#define IRC_LOGIC_ONE_TIME_LOGIC_ONE_MARK_7                     ((uint32_t)0x00008000U) /* Bit 7 */
#define IRC_LOGIC_ONE_TIME_LOGIC_ONE_MARK_MASK                  ((uint32_t)0x0000FF00U) /* field mask */

#define IRC_LOGIC_ONE_TIME_LOGIC_ONE_SPACE                      ((uint32_t)0x000000FFU) /* Bit[7:0] Logic 1 SPACE time (carrier clock cycle) */
#define IRC_LOGIC_ONE_TIME_LOGIC_ONE_SPACE_0                    ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit 0 */
#define IRC_LOGIC_ONE_TIME_LOGIC_ONE_SPACE_1                    ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit 1 */
#define IRC_LOGIC_ONE_TIME_LOGIC_ONE_SPACE_2                    ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit 2 */
#define IRC_LOGIC_ONE_TIME_LOGIC_ONE_SPACE_3                    ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit 3 */
#define IRC_LOGIC_ONE_TIME_LOGIC_ONE_SPACE_4                    ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit 4 */
#define IRC_LOGIC_ONE_TIME_LOGIC_ONE_SPACE_5                    ((uint32_t)0x00000020U) /* Bit 5 */
#define IRC_LOGIC_ONE_TIME_LOGIC_ONE_SPACE_6                    ((uint32_t)0x00000040U) /* Bit 6 */
#define IRC_LOGIC_ONE_TIME_LOGIC_ONE_SPACE_7                    ((uint32_t)0x00000080U) /* Bit 7 */
#define IRC_LOGIC_ONE_TIME_LOGIC_ONE_SPACE_MASK                 ((uint32_t)0x000000FFU) /* field mask */


/** Bit definition for IRC_LOGIC_ZERO_TIME register **/

#define IRC_LOGIC_ZERO_TIME_LOGIC_ZERO_MARK_1                   ((uint32_t)0x00000200U) /* Bit 1 */

#define IRC_LOGIC_ZERO_TIME_LOGIC_ZERO_MARK_4                   ((uint32_t)0x00001000U) /* Bit 4 */
#define IRC_LOGIC_ZERO_TIME_LOGIC_ZERO_MARK_5                   ((uint32_t)0x00002000U) /* Bit 5 */

#define IRC_LOGIC_ZERO_TIME_LOGIC_ZERO_SPACE                    ((uint32_t)0x000000FFU) /* Bit[7:0] Logic 0 SPACE time (carrier clock cycle) */
#define IRC_LOGIC_ZERO_TIME_LOGIC_ZERO_SPACE_0                  ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit 0 */
#define IRC_LOGIC_ZERO_TIME_LOGIC_ZERO_SPACE_1                  ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit 1 */
#define IRC_LOGIC_ZERO_TIME_LOGIC_ZERO_SPACE_2                  ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit 2 */
#define IRC_LOGIC_ZERO_TIME_LOGIC_ZERO_SPACE_3                  ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit 3 */
#define IRC_LOGIC_ZERO_TIME_LOGIC_ZERO_SPACE_4                  ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit 4 */

/** Bit definition for IRC_CTRL register **/
#define IRC_CTRL_NO_CARR_MODE                                   ((uint32_t)0x00000200U) /* Bit9 Carrier-free mode selection */

#define IRC_CTRL_IR_IRQ_EN                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000100U) /* Bit8 IRC module interrupt enable selection */

#define IRC_CTRL_IR_LOGIC_ONE_FORMAT                            ((uint32_t)0x00000080U) /* Bit7 Logic 1 format */

#define IRC_CTRL_IR_LOGIC_ZERO_FORMAT                           ((uint32_t)0x00000040U) /* Bit6 Logical 0 format */

#define IRC_CTRL_IR_INVERT_OUTPUT                               ((uint32_t)0x00000020U) /* Bit5 Infrared transmission signal reverse output enable */

#define IRC_CTRL_IR_TX_START                                    ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit3 IR transmission control bit */

#define IRC_CTRL_IR_ENABLE                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit2 IR module enable selection */

#define IRC_CTRL_IR_REP_FIFO_RESET                              ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit1 REPEAT FIFO clear */

#define IRC_CTRL_IR_CODE_FIFO_RESET                             ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit0 CODE FIFO clear */


/** Bit definition for IRC_STATUS register **/
#define IRC_STATUS_IR_IRQ_FLG                                   ((uint32_t)0x00000800U) /* Bit11 Transmission completion flag */

#define IRC_STATUS_IR_BUSY                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000400U) /* Bit10 IRC busy flag */

#define IRC_STATUS_IR_REP_FIFO_WORDS                            ((uint32_t)0x000003C0U) /* Bit[9:6] Number of words in REPEAT FIFO */
#define IRC_STATUS_IR_REP_FIFO_WORDS_0                          ((uint32_t)0x00000040U) /* Bit 0 */
#define IRC_STATUS_IR_REP_FIFO_WORDS_1                          ((uint32_t)0x00000080U) /* Bit 1 */
#define IRC_STATUS_IR_REP_FIFO_WORDS_2                          ((uint32_t)0x00000100U) /* Bit 2 */
#define IRC_STATUS_IR_REP_FIFO_WORDS_3                          ((uint32_t)0x00000200U) /* Bit 3 */
#define IRC_STATUS_IR_REP_FIFO_WORDS_MASK                       ((uint32_t)0x000003C0U) /* field mask */

#define IRC_STATUS_IR_CODE_FIFO_WORDS                           ((uint32_t)0x0000003FU) /* Bit[5:0] Number of words in CODE FIFO */
#define IRC_STATUS_IR_CODE_FIFO_WORDS_0                         ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit 0 */
#define IRC_STATUS_IR_CODE_FIFO_WORDS_1                         ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit 1 */
#define IRC_STATUS_IR_CODE_FIFO_WORDS_2                         ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit 2 */
#define IRC_STATUS_IR_CODE_FIFO_WORDS_3                         ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit 3 */
#define IRC_STATUS_IR_CODE_FIFO_WORDS_4                         ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit 4 */
#define IRC_STATUS_IR_CODE_FIFO_WORDS_5                         ((uint32_t)0x00000020U) /* Bit 5 */
#define IRC_STATUS_IR_CODE_FIFO_WORDS_MASK                      ((uint32_t)0x0000003FU) /* field mask */


/** Bit definition for IRC_REPEAT_TIME register **/
#define IRC_REPEAT_TIME_IR_REPEAT_TIME                          ((uint32_t)0x0000FFFFU) /* Bit[15:0] Define the repetition time (number of carrier clock cycles). */
#define IRC_REPEAT_TIME_IR_REPEAT_TIME_0                        ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit 0 */
#define IRC_REPEAT_TIME_IR_REPEAT_TIME_1                        ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit 1 */
#define IRC_REPEAT_TIME_IR_REPEAT_TIME_2                        ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit 2 */
#define IRC_REPEAT_TIME_IR_REPEAT_TIME_3                        ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit 3 */
#define IRC_REPEAT_TIME_IR_REPEAT_TIME_4                        ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit 4 */
#define IRC_REPEAT_TIME_IR_REPEAT_TIME_5                        ((uint32_t)0x00000020U) /* Bit 5 */
#define IRC_REPEAT_TIME_IR_REPEAT_TIME_6                        ((uint32_t)0x00000040U) /* Bit 6 */
#define IRC_REPEAT_TIME_IR_REPEAT_TIME_7                        ((uint32_t)0x00000080U) /* Bit 7 */
#define IRC_REPEAT_TIME_IR_REPEAT_TIME_8                        ((uint32_t)0x00000100U) /* Bit 8 */
#define IRC_REPEAT_TIME_IR_REPEAT_TIME_9                        ((uint32_t)0x00000200U) /* Bit 9 */
#define IRC_REPEAT_TIME_IR_REPEAT_TIME_10                       ((uint32_t)0x00000400U) /* Bit 10 */
#define IRC_REPEAT_TIME_IR_REPEAT_TIME_11                       ((uint32_t)0x00000800U) /* Bit 11 */
#define IRC_REPEAT_TIME_IR_REPEAT_TIME_12                       ((uint32_t)0x00001000U) /* Bit 12 */
#define IRC_REPEAT_TIME_IR_REPEAT_TIME_13                       ((uint32_t)0x00002000U) /* Bit 13 */
#define IRC_REPEAT_TIME_IR_REPEAT_TIME_14                       ((uint32_t)0x00004000U) /* Bit 14 */
#define IRC_REPEAT_TIME_IR_REPEAT_TIME_15                       ((uint32_t)0x00008000U) /* Bit 15 */
#define IRC_REPEAT_TIME_IR_REPEAT_TIME_MASK                     ((uint32_t)0x0000FFFFU) /* field mask */


/** Bit definition for IRC_CODE_FIFO register **/

#define IRC_CODE_FIFO_IR_CODE_FIFO_DATA_8                       ((uint32_t)0x00000100U) /* Bit 8 */

#define IRC_CODE_FIFO_IR_CODE_FIFO_DATA_10                      ((uint32_t)0x00000400U) /* Bit 10 */
#define IRC_CODE_FIFO_IR_CODE_FIFO_DATA_11                      ((uint32_t)0x00000800U) /* Bit 11 */
#define IRC_CODE_FIFO_IR_CODE_FIFO_DATA_12                      ((uint32_t)0x00001000U) /* Bit 12 */
#define IRC_CODE_FIFO_IR_CODE_FIFO_DATA_13                      ((uint32_t)0x00002000U) /* Bit 13 */
#define IRC_CODE_FIFO_IR_CODE_FIFO_DATA_14                      ((uint32_t)0x00004000U) /* Bit 14 */
#define IRC_CODE_FIFO_IR_CODE_FIFO_DATA_15                      ((uint32_t)0x00008000U) /* Bit 15 */
#define IRC_CODE_FIFO_IR_CODE_FIFO_DATA_16                      ((uint32_t)0x00010000U) /* Bit 16 */
#define IRC_CODE_FIFO_IR_CODE_FIFO_DATA_17                      ((uint32_t)0x00020000U) /* Bit 17 */
