#define IRC_REPEAT_FIFO_IR_REPEAT_FIFO_DATA_3                   ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit 3 */
#define IRC_REPEAT_FIFO_IR_REPEAT_FIFO_DATA_4                   ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit 4 */
#define IRC_REPEAT_FIFO_IR_REPEAT_FIFO_DATA_5                   ((uint32_t)0x00000020U) /* Bit 5 */
#define IRC_REPEAT_FIFO_IR_REPEAT_FIFO_DATA_6                   ((uint32_t)0x00000040U) /* Bit 6 */
#define IRC_REPEAT_FIFO_IR_REPEAT_FIFO_DATA_7                   ((uint32_t)0x00000080U) /* Bit 7 */
#define IRC_REPEAT_FIFO_IR_REPEAT_FIFO_DATA_8                   ((uint32_t)0x00000100U) /* Bit 8 */
#define IRC_REPEAT_FIFO_IR_REPEAT_FIFO_DATA_9                   ((uint32_t)0x00000200U) /* Bit 9 */
#define IRC_REPEAT_FIFO_IR_REPEAT_FIFO_DATA_10                  ((uint32_t)0x00000400U) /* Bit 10 */
#define IRC_REPEAT_FIFO_IR_REPEAT_FIFO_DATA_11                  ((uint32_t)0x00000800U) /* Bit 11 */
#define IRC_REPEAT_FIFO_IR_REPEAT_FIFO_DATA_12                  ((uint32_t)0x00001000U) /* Bit 12 */
#define IRC_REPEAT_FIFO_IR_REPEAT_FIFO_DATA_13                  ((uint32_t)0x00002000U) /* Bit 13 */
#define IRC_REPEAT_FIFO_IR_REPEAT_FIFO_DATA_14                  ((uint32_t)0x00004000U) /* Bit 14 */
#define IRC_REPEAT_FIFO_IR_REPEAT_FIFO_DATA_15                  ((uint32_t)0x00008000U) /* Bit 15 */
#define IRC_REPEAT_FIFO_IR_REPEAT_FIFO_DATA_16                  ((uint32_t)0x00010000U) /* Bit 16 */
#define IRC_REPEAT_FIFO_IR_REPEAT_FIFO_DATA_17                  ((uint32_t)0x00020000U) /* Bit 17 */
#define IRC_REPEAT_FIFO_IR_REPEAT_FIFO_DATA_18                  ((uint32_t)0x00040000U) /* Bit 18 */
#define IRC_REPEAT_FIFO_IR_REPEAT_FIFO_DATA_19                  ((uint32_t)0x00080000U) /* Bit 19 */
#define IRC_REPEAT_FIFO_IR_REPEAT_FIFO_DATA_20                  ((uint32_t)0x00100000U) /* Bit 20 */
#define IRC_REPEAT_FIFO_IR_REPEAT_FIFO_DATA_MASK                ((uint32_t)0x001FFFFFU) /* field mask */


/********************  Bits definition for FREQ_CARRIER_ON register  *******************/
#define IR_FREQ_CARR_ON_Msk       ((uint32_t)0x000007FFU)

/********************  Bits definition for FREQ_CARRIER_OFF register  *******************/
#define IR_FREQ_CARR_OFF_Msk      ((uint32_t)0x000007FFU)

/********************  Bits definition for LOGIC_ONE_TIME register  *******************/
#define IR_LOGIC_ONE_TIME_LOGIC_ONE_SPACE_Pos   (0U)
#define IR_LOGIC_ONE_TIME_LOGIC_ONE_SPACE_Msk   ((uint32_t)0x000000FFU)
#define IR_LOGIC_ONE_TIME_LOGIC_ONE_MARK_Pos    (8U)
#define IR_LOGIC_ONE_TIME_LOGIC_ONE_MARK_Msk    ((uint32_t)0x0000FF00U)

/********************  Bits definition for LOGIC_ZERO_TIME register  *******************/
#define IR_LOGIC_ZERO_TIME_LOGIC_ZERO_SPACE_Pos (0U)
#define IR_LOGIC_ZERO_TIME_LOGIC_ZERO_SPACE_Msk ((uint32_t)0x000000FFU)
#define IR_LOGIC_ZERO_TIME_LOGIC_ZERO_MARK_Pos  (8U)
#define IR_LOGIC_ZERO_TIME_LOGIC_ZERO_MARK_Msk  ((uint32_t)0x0000FF00U)

/********************  Bits definition for IR_CTRL register  *******************/
#define IR_CTRL_CODE_FIFO_RESET_Pos     (0U)
#define IR_CTRL_CODE_FIFO_RESET_Msk     ((uint32_t)0x00000001U)
#define IR_CTRL_REP_FIFO_RESET_Pos      (1U)
#define IR_CTRL_REP_FIFO_RESET_Msk      ((uint32_t)0x00000002U)
#define IR_CTRL_ENABLE_Pos              (2U)
#define IR_CTRL_ENABLE_Msk              ((uint32_t)0x00000004U)
#define IR_CTRL_TX_START_Pos            (3U)
#define IR_CTRL_TX_START_Msk            ((uint32_t)0x00000008U)
#define IR_CTRL_INVERT_OUTPUT_Pos       (5U)
#define IR_CTRL_INVERT_OUTPUT_Msk       ((uint32_t)0x00000020U)
#define IR_CTRL_LOGIC_ZERO_FORMAT_Pos   (6U)
#define IR_CTRL_LOGIC_ZERO_FORMAT_Msk   ((uint32_t)0x00000040U)
#define IR_CTRL_LOGIC_ONE_FORMAT_Pos    (7U)
#define IR_CTRL_LOGIC_ONE_FORMAT_Msk    ((uint32_t)0x00000080U)
#define IR_CTRL_IRQ_EN_Pos              (8U)
#define IR_CTRL_IRQ_EN_Msk              ((uint32_t)0x00000100U)

/********************  Bits definition for IR_STATUS register  *******************/
#define IR_STATUS_CODE_FIFO_WORDS_Pos   (0U)
#define IR_STATUS_CODE_FIFO_WORDS_Msk   ((uint32_t)0x0000003FU)
#define IR_STATUS_REP_FIFO_WORDS_Pos    (6U)
#define IR_STATUS_REP_FIFO_WORDS_Msk    ((uint32_t)0x000003C0U)
#define IR_STATUS_BUSY                  ((uint32_t)0x00000400U)
#define IR_IRQ_FLAG                     ((uint32_t)0x00000800U)

/********************  Bits definition for IR_REPEAT_TIME register  *******************/
#define IR_REPEAT_TIME_Msk              ((uint32_t)0x0000FFFFU)

/********************  Bits definition for IR_CODE_FIFO register  *******************/
#define IR_CODE_FIFO_Msk                ((uint32_t)0x001FFFFFU)

/********************  Bits definition for IR_REPEAT_FIFO register  *******************/
#define IR_REPEAT_FIFO_Msk              ((uint32_t)0x001FFFFFU)

/******************************************************************************/
/*               Peripheral Registers Bit Definition -- I2C                   */
/******************************************************************************/

#define I2C1_BASE  ((uint32_t)(APB1PERIPH_BASE + (uint32_t)0x5400U))
#define I2C2_BASE  ((uint32_t)(APB1PERIPH_BASE + (uint32_t)0x5800U))

/** Inter Integrated Circuit Interface **/
typedef struct
{
    __IO uint32_t CTRL1;          //0x00
    __IO uint32_t CTRL2;          //0x04
    __IO uint16_t OADDR1;         //0x08
    uint16_t  RESERVED1;
    __IO uint16_t OADDR2;         //0x0C
    uint16_t  RESERVED2;
    __IO uint16_t DAT;            //0x10
    uint16_t  RESERVED3; 
    __IO uint32_t STS1;           //0x14
    __IO uint32_t STS2;           //0x18
    __IO uint16_t CLKCTRL;        //0x1C
    uint16_t  RESERVED4;
    __IO uint16_t TMRISE;         //0x20
    uint16_t  RESERVED5; 
    uint32_t RESERVED6;           //0x24
    __IO uint16_t GFLTRCTRL;      //0x28
    uint16_t  RESERVED10; 
} I2C_Module;

#define I2C1  ((I2C_Module*)(uintptr_t)I2C1_BASE)
#define I2C2  ((I2C_Module*)(uintptr_t)I2C2_BASE)

/** Bit definition for I2C_CTRL1 register **/
#define I2C_CTRL1_LTC                                           ((uint32_t)0x60000000U) /* Bit[30:29] Low timeout threshold selection */
#define I2C_CTRL1_LTC_0                                         ((uint32_t)0x20000000U) /* Bit 0 */
#define I2C_CTRL1_LTC_1                                         ((uint32_t)0x40000000U) /* Bit 1 */
#define I2C_CTRL1_LTC_MASK                                      ((uint32_t)0x60000000U) /* field mask */

#define I2C_CTRL1_HTC                                           ((uint32_t)0x18000000U) /* Bit[28:27] High timeout threshold selection */
#define I2C_CTRL1_HTC_0                                         ((uint32_t)0x08000000U) /* Bit 0 */
#define I2C_CTRL1_HTC_1                                         ((uint32_t)0x10000000U) /* Bit 1 */
#define I2C_CTRL1_HTC_MASK                                      ((uint32_t)0x18000000U) /* field mask */

#define I2C_CTRL1_DATACKTC                                      ((uint32_t)0x00080000U) /* Bit19 Data ACK timing control in slave receive mode */

#define I2C_CTRL1_SWRESET                                       ((uint32_t)0x00002000U) /* Bit13 Software reset */

#define I2C_CTRL1_SMBALERT                                      ((uint32_t)0x00001000U) /* Bit12 SMBus alert */

#define I2C_CTRL1_PEC                                           ((uint32_t)0x00000800U) /* Bit11 Packet error checking */

#define I2C_CTRL1_ACKPOS                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000400U) /* Bit10 ACK/PEC position for data reception */

#define I2C_CTRL1_ACKEN                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000200U) /* Bit9 Acknowledge enable */

#define I2C_CTRL1_STOPGEN                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000100U) /* Bit8 Stop generation */

#define I2C_CTRL1_STARTGEN                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000080U) /* Bit7 Start generation */

#define I2C_CTRL1_NOEXTEND                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000040U) /* Bit6 Clock stretching disable in slave mode */

#define I2C_CTRL1_GCEN                                          ((uint32_t)0x00000020U) /* Bit5 General call enable */

#define I2C_CTRL1_PECEN                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit4 PEC enable */

#define I2C_CTRL1_ARPEN                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit3 ARP enable */

#define I2C_CTRL1_SMBTYPE                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit2 SMBus type */

#define I2C_CTRL1_SMBMODE                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit1 SMBus mode */

#define I2C_CTRL1_EN                                            ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit0 Peripheral enable */


/** Bit definition for I2C_CTRL2 register **/
#define I2C_CTRL2_TOLEN                                         ((uint32_t)0x40000000U) /* Bit30 SCL and SDA low timeout enable */

#define I2C_CTRL2_TOHEN                                         ((uint32_t)0x20000000U) /* Bit29 High timeout enable */

#define I2C_CTRL2_SCLTOLIEN                                     ((uint32_t)0x10000000U) /* Bit28 SCL low timeout interrupt enable */

#define I2C_CTRL2_SCLTOHIEN                                     ((uint32_t)0x08000000U) /* Bit27 SCL high timeout interrupt enable */

#define I2C_CTRL2_SDATOLIEN                                     ((uint32_t)0x04000000U) /* Bit26 SDA low timeout interrupt enable */

#define I2C_CTRL2_DMAEN                                         ((uint32_t)0x00008000U) /* Bit15 DMA requests enable */

#define I2C_CTRL2_ERRINTEN                                      ((uint32_t)0x00004000U) /* Bit14 Error interrupt enable */

#define I2C_CTRL2_EVTINTEN                                      ((uint32_t)0x00002000U) /* Bit13 Event interrupt enable */

#define I2C_CTRL2_BUFINTEN                                      ((uint32_t)0x00001000U) /* Bit12 Buffer interrupt enable */

#define I2C_CTRL2_DMALAST                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000100U) /* Bit8 DMA last transfer */

#define I2C_CTRL2_CLKFREQ                                       ((uint32_t)0x0000007FU) /* Bit[6:0] Peripheral clock frequency */
#define I2C_CTRL2_CLKFREQ_0                                     ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit 0 */
#define I2C_CTRL2_CLKFREQ_1                                     ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit 1 */
#define I2C_CTRL2_CLKFREQ_2                                     ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit 2 */
#define I2C_CTRL2_CLKFREQ_3                                     ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit 3 */
#define I2C_CTRL2_CLKFREQ_4                                     ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit 4 */
#define I2C_CTRL2_CLKFREQ_5                                     ((uint32_t)0x00000020U) /* Bit 5 */
#define I2C_CTRL2_CLKFREQ_6                                     ((uint32_t)0x00000040U) /* Bit 6 */
#define I2C_CTRL2_CLKFREQ_MASK                                  ((uint32_t)0x0000007FU) /* field mask */


/** Bit definition for I2C_OADDR1 register **/
#define I2C_OADDR1_ADDRMODE                                     ((uint16_t)0x8000U) /* Bit15 Addressing mode in slave mode */

#define I2C_OADDR1_ADDR                                         ((uint16_t)0x03FFU) /* Bit[9:0] Interface address 9:0 */
#define I2C_OADDR1_ADDR_0                                       ((uint16_t)0x0001U) /* Bit 0 */
#define I2C_OADDR1_ADDR_1                                       ((uint16_t)0x0002U) /* Bit 1 */
#define I2C_OADDR1_ADDR_2                                       ((uint16_t)0x0004U) /* Bit 2 */
#define I2C_OADDR1_ADDR_3                                       ((uint16_t)0x0008U) /* Bit 3 */
#define I2C_OADDR1_ADDR_4                                       ((uint16_t)0x0010U) /* Bit 4 */
#define I2C_OADDR1_ADDR_5                                       ((uint16_t)0x0020U) /* Bit 5 */
#define I2C_OADDR1_ADDR_6                                       ((uint16_t)0x0040U) /* Bit 6 */
#define I2C_OADDR1_ADDR_7                                       ((uint16_t)0x0080U) /* Bit 7 */
#define I2C_OADDR1_ADDR_8                                       ((uint16_t)0x0100U) /* Bit 8 */
#define I2C_OADDR1_ADDR_9                                       ((uint16_t)0x0200U) /* Bit 9 */
#define I2C_OADDR1_ADDR_MASK                                    ((uint16_t)0x03FFU) /* field mask */


/** Bit definition for I2C_OADDR2 register **/
#define I2C_OADDR2_ADDR2                                        ((uint16_t)0x00FEU) /* Bit[7:1] Interface address 7:1 in dual addressing mode */
#define I2C_OADDR2_ADDR2_0                                      ((uint16_t)0x0002U) /* Bit 0 */
#define I2C_OADDR2_ADDR2_1                                      ((uint16_t)0x0004U) /* Bit 1 */
#define I2C_OADDR2_ADDR2_2                                      ((uint16_t)0x0008U) /* Bit 2 */
#define I2C_OADDR2_ADDR2_3                                      ((uint16_t)0x0010U) /* Bit 3 */
#define I2C_OADDR2_ADDR2_4                                      ((uint16_t)0x0020U) /* Bit 4 */
#define I2C_OADDR2_ADDR2_5                                      ((uint16_t)0x0040U) /* Bit 5 */
#define I2C_OADDR2_ADDR2_6                                      ((uint16_t)0x0080U) /* Bit 6 */
#define I2C_OADDR2_ADDR2_MASK                                   ((uint16_t)0x00FEU) /* field mask */

#define I2C_OADDR2_DUALEN                                       ((uint16_t)0x0001U) /* Bit0 Dual addressing mode enable */


/** Bit definition for I2C_DAT register **/
#define I2C_DAT_DATA                                            ((uint16_t)0x00FFU) /* Bit[7:0] 8-bit data register */
#define I2C_DAT_DATA_0                                          ((uint16_t)0x0001U) /* Bit 0 */
#define I2C_DAT_DATA_1                                          ((uint16_t)0x0002U) /* Bit 1 */
#define I2C_DAT_DATA_2                                          ((uint16_t)0x0004U) /* Bit 2 */
#define I2C_DAT_DATA_3                                          ((uint16_t)0x0008U) /* Bit 3 */
#define I2C_DAT_DATA_4                                          ((uint16_t)0x0010U) /* Bit 4 */
#define I2C_DAT_DATA_5                                          ((uint16_t)0x0020U) /* Bit 5 */
#define I2C_DAT_DATA_6                                          ((uint16_t)0x0040U) /* Bit 6 */
#define I2C_DAT_DATA_7                                          ((uint16_t)0x0080U) /* Bit 7 */
#define I2C_DAT_DATA_MASK                                       ((uint16_t)0x00FFU) /* field mask */


/** Bit definition for I2C_STS1 register **/
#define I2C_STS1_SCLLTO                                         ((uint32_t)0x02000000U) /* Bit25 SCL low timeout error */

#define I2C_STS1_SCLHTO                                         ((uint32_t)0x01000000U) /* Bit24 SCL high timeout error */

#define I2C_STS1_SDALTO                                         ((uint32_t)0x00800000U) /* Bit23 SDA low timeout error */

#define I2C_STS1_SMBALERT                                       ((uint32_t)0x00004000U) /* Bit14 SMBus alert */

#define I2C_STS1_TIMEOUT                                        ((uint32_t)0x00002000U) /* Bit13 Timeout flag */

#define I2C_STS1_PECERR                                         ((uint32_t)0x00001000U) /* Bit12 PEC Error in reception */

#define I2C_STS1_OVERRUN                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000800U) /* Bit11 Overrun/Underrun */

#define I2C_STS1_BUSERR                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000400U) /* Bit10 Bus error */

#define I2C_STS1_ARLOST                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000200U) /* Bit9 Arbitration lost in master mode */

#define I2C_STS1_ACKFAIL                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000100U) /* Bit8 Acknowledge failure */

#define I2C_STS1_ADDR10F                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000040U) /* Bit6 10-bit header sequence sent in master mode */

#define I2C_STS1_TXDATE                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000020U) /* Bit5 Data register empty in transmission */

#define I2C_STS1_RXDATNE                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit4 Data register not empty in reception */

#define I2C_STS1_STOPF                                          ((uint32_t)0x00000008U) /* Bit3 Stop detection flag in slave mode */

#define I2C_STS1_BSF                                            ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit2 Byte transfer finished */

#define I2C_STS1_ADDRF                                          ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit1 Address sent in master mode or address matched in slave mode */

#define I2C_STS1_STARTBF                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit0 Start bit flag in master mode */


/** Bit definition for I2C_STS2 register **/
#define I2C_STS2_PECVAL                                         ((uint32_t)0x0000FF00U) /* Bit[15:8] Packet error checking value */
#define I2C_STS2_PECVAL_0                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000100U) /* Bit 0 */
#define I2C_STS2_PECVAL_1                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000200U) /* Bit 1 */
#define I2C_STS2_PECVAL_2                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000400U) /* Bit 2 */
#define I2C_STS2_PECVAL_3                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000800U) /* Bit 3 */
#define I2C_STS2_PECVAL_4                                       ((uint32_t)0x00001000U) /* Bit 4 */
#define I2C_STS2_PECVAL_5                                       ((uint32_t)0x00002000U) /* Bit 5 */
#define I2C_STS2_PECVAL_6                                       ((uint32_t)0x00004000U) /* Bit 6 */
#define I2C_STS2_PECVAL_7                                       ((uint32_t)0x00008000U) /* Bit 7 */
#define I2C_STS2_PECVAL_MASK                                    ((uint32_t)0x0000FF00U) /* field mask */

#define I2C_STS2_SMBHADDR                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000080U) /* Bit7 SMBus host header address in slave mode */

#define I2C_STS2_SMBDADDR                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000040U) /* Bit6 SMBus device default address in slave mode */

#define I2C_STS2_DUALFLAG                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000020U) /* Bit5 Dual flag in slave mode */

#define I2C_STS2_GCALLADDR                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000010U) /* Bit4 General call address in slave mode */

#define I2C_STS2_TRF                                            ((uint32_t)0x00000004U) /* Bit2 Transmitter/receiver */

#define I2C_STS2_MSMODE                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000002U) /* Bit1 Master/slave mode */

#define I2C_STS2_BUSY                                           ((uint32_t)0x00000001U) /* Bit0 Bus busy */


/** Bit definition for I2C_CLKCTRL register **/
#define I2C_CLKCTRL_DUTY                                        ((uint16_t)0x8000U) /* Bit15 Fast mode duty cycle */

#define I2C_CLKCTRL_FSMODE                                      ((uint16_t)0x4000U) /* Bit14 I2C master mode selection */

#define I2C_CLKCTRL_CLKCTRL                                     ((uint16_t)0x0FFFU) /* Bit[11:0] Clock control divider in fast/standard mode */
#define I2C_CLKCTRL_CLKCTRL_0                                   ((uint16_t)0x0001U) /* Bit 0 */
#define I2C_CLKCTRL_CLKCTRL_1                                   ((uint16_t)0x0002U) /* Bit 1 */
#define I2C_CLKCTRL_CLKCTRL_2                                   ((uint16_t)0x0004U) /* Bit 2 */
#define I2C_CLKCTRL_CLKCTRL_3                                   ((uint16_t)0x0008U) /* Bit 3 */
#define I2C_CLKCTRL_CLKCTRL_4                                   ((uint16_t)0x0010U) /* Bit 4 */
#define I2C_CLKCTRL_CLKCTRL_5                                   ((uint16_t)0x0020U) /* Bit 5 */
#define I2C_CLKCTRL_CLKCTRL_6                                   ((uint16_t)0x0040U) /* Bit 6 */
#define I2C_CLKCTRL_CLKCTRL_7                                   ((uint16_t)0x0080U) /* Bit 7 */
#define I2C_CLKCTRL_CLKCTRL_8                                   ((uint16_t)0x0100U) /* Bit 8 */
#define I2C_CLKCTRL_CLKCTRL_9                                   ((uint16_t)0x0200U) /* Bit 9 */
#define I2C_CLKCTRL_CLKCTRL_10                                  ((uint16_t)0x0400U) /* Bit 10 */
#define I2C_CLKCTRL_CLKCTRL_11                                  ((uint16_t)0x0800U) /* Bit 11 */
#define I2C_CLKCTRL_CLKCTRL_MASK                                ((uint16_t)0x0FFFU) /* field mask */


/** Bit definition for I2C_TMRISE register **/
#define I2C_TMRISE_TMRISE                                       ((uint16_t)0x003FU) /* Bit[5:0] Maximum rise time in fast/standard mode */
#define I2C_TMRISE_TMRISE_0                                     ((uint16_t)0x0001U) /* Bit 0 */
#define I2C_TMRISE_TMRISE_1                                     ((uint16_t)0x0002U) /* Bit 1 */

/** Bit definition for I2C_GFLTRCTRL register **/
#define I2C_GFLTRCTRL_SCLAFENN                                  ((uint16_t)0x8000U) /* Bit15 SCL analog filter enable */

#define I2C_GFLTRCTRL_SCLAFW                                    ((uint16_t)0x3000U) /* Bit[13:12] SCL analog filter width selection */

#define I2C_GFLTRCTRL_SCLAFW_1                                  ((uint16_t)0x2000U) /* Bit 1 */
#define I2C_GFLTRCTRL_SCLAFW_MASK                               ((uint16_t)0x3000U) /* field mask */

#define I2C_GFLTRCTRL_SDAAFENN                                  ((uint16_t)0x0800U) /* Bit11 SDA analog filter enable */

#define I2C_GFLTRCTRL_SDAAFW                                    ((uint16_t)0x0300U) /* Bit[9:8] SDA analog filter width selection */
#define I2C_GFLTRCTRL_SDAAFW_0                                  ((uint16_t)0x0100U) /* Bit 0 */
#define I2C_GFLTRCTRL_SDAAFW_1                                  ((uint16_t)0x0200U) /* Bit 1 */
#define I2C_GFLTRCTRL_SDAAFW_MASK                               ((uint16_t)0x0300U) /* field mask */

#define I2C_GFLTRCTRL_SCLDFW                                    ((uint16_t)0x00F0U) /* Bit[7:4] SCL digital filter width selection */
#define I2C_GFLTRCTRL_SCLDFW_0                                  ((uint16_t)0x0010U) /* Bit 0 */
#define I2C_GFLTRCTRL_SCLDFW_1                                  ((uint16_t)0x0020U) /* Bit 1 */
#define I2C_GFLTRCTRL_SCLDFW_2                                  ((uint16_t)0x0040U) /* Bit 2 */
#define I2C_GFLTRCTRL_SCLDFW_3                                  ((uint16_t)0x0080U) /* Bit 3 */
#define I2C_GFLTRCTRL_SCLDFW_MASK                               ((uint16_t)0x00F0U) /* field mask */

#define I2C_GFLTRCTRL_SDADFW                                    ((uint16_t)0x000FU) /* Bit[3:0] SDA digital filter width selection */
#define I2C_GFLTRCTRL_SDADFW_0                                  ((uint16_t)0x0001U) /* Bit 0 */
#define I2C_GFLTRCTRL_SDADFW_1                                  ((uint16_t)0x0002U) /* Bit 1 */
#define I2C_GFLTRCTRL_SDADFW_2                                  ((uint16_t)0x0004U) /* Bit 2 */
#define I2C_GFLTRCTRL_SDADFW_3                                  ((uint16_t)0x0008U) /* Bit 3 */
#define I2C_GFLTRCTRL_SDADFW_MASK                               ((uint16_t)0x000FU) /* field mask */



/******************************************************************************/
/*               Peripheral Registers Bit Definition -- CRC                   */
/******************************************************************************/

#define CRC_BASE  ((uint32_t)(AHBPERIPH_BASE + (uint32_t)0x3000U))

/*** CRC ***/
typedef struct {
    __IO uint32_t CRC32DAT;   //0x00
    __IO uint32_t CRC32IDAT;  //0x04
    __IO uint32_t CRC32CTRL;  //0x08
    __IO uint32_t CRC16CTRL;  //0x0C
    __IO uint32_t CRC16DAT;   //0x10
    __IO uint32_t CRC16D;     //0x14
    __IO uint32_t LRC;        //0x18
} CRC_Module;

#define CRC        ((CRC_Module*)(uintptr_t)CRC_BASE)

/*******************  Bit definition for CRC_CRC32DAT register  *********************/
#define CRC32_DAT_DAT ((uint32_t)0xFFFFFFFFU) /*!< Data register bits */

/*******************  Bit definition for CRC_CRC32IDAT register  ********************/
#define CRC32_IDAT_IDAT ((uint8_t)0xFFU) /*!< General-purpose 8-bit data register bits */

/********************  Bit definition for CRC_CRC32CTRL register  ********************/
#define CRC32_CTRL_RESET ((uint8_t)0x01U) /*!< RESET bit */

/********************  Bit definition for CRC16_CR register  ********************/
#define CRC16_CTRL_LITTLE ((uint8_t)0x02U)
#define CRC16_CTRL_BIG    ((uint8_t)0xFDU)

#define CRC16_CTRL_RESET    ((uint8_t)0x04U)
#define CRC16_CTRL_NO_RESET ((uint8_t)0xFBU)


/******************************************************************************/
/*               Peripheral Registers Bit Definition -- ADC                   */
/******************************************************************************/

#define ADC_BASE  ((uint32_t)(AHBPERIPH_BASE + (uint32_t)0x0800U))

typedef struct
{
    __IO uint32_t STS;       //0x00
    __IO uint32_t CTRL1;     //0x04
    __IO uint32_t CTRL2;     //0x08
    __IO uint32_t SAMPT1;    //0x0C
    __IO uint32_t SAMPT2;    //0x10
    __IO uint32_t OFFSET[4]; //0x14-0x20
    __IO uint32_t AWDHIGH;   //0x24
    __IO uint32_t AWDLOW;    //0x28
    __IO uint32_t RSEQ1;     //0x2C
    __IO uint32_t RSEQ2;     //0x30
    __IO uint32_t RSEQ3;     //0x34
    __IO uint32_t JSEQ;      //0x38
    __IO uint32_t JDAT[4];   //0x3C-0x48
    __IO uint32_t DAT;       //0x4C
    __IO uint32_t DIFSEL;    //0x50
    __IO uint32_t CALFACT;   //0x54
    __IO uint32_t CTRL3;     //0x58
    __IO uint32_t SAMPT3;    //0x5C
    __IO uint32_t PCTRL;     //0x60
} ADC_Module;

#define ADC  ((ADC_Module*)(uintptr_t)ADC_BASE)

/******** Bit definition for ADC_STS register ********/
#define ADC_STS_AWDG                                           ((uint32_t)0x00000001U)  /* bit 0 */

#define ADC_STS_ENDC                                           ((uint32_t)0x00000002U)  /* bit 1 */

#define ADC_STS_JENDC                                          ((uint32_t)0x00000004U)  /* bit 2 */

#define ADC_STS_JSTR                                           ((uint32_t)0x00000008U)  /* bit 3 */

#define ADC_STS_STR                                            ((uint32_t)0x00000010U)  /* bit 4 */

#define ADC_STS_ENDCA                                          ((uint32_t)0x00000020U)  /* bit 5 */

#define ADC_STS_JENDCA                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000040U)  /* bit 6 */

#define ADC_STS_TCFLAG                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000080U)  /* bit 7 */


/******** Bit definition for ADC_CTRL1 register ********/
#define ADC_CTRL1_AWDCH                                        ((uint32_t)0x0000001FU)  /* bits [4:0] */
#define ADC_CTRL1_AWDCH_MASK                                   ((uint32_t)0x0000001FU)  /* bits [4:0] field mask */
#define ADC_CTRL1_AWDCH_0                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000001U)  /* bit 0 */
#define ADC_CTRL1_AWDCH_1                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000002U)  /* bit 1 */
#define ADC_CTRL1_AWDCH_2                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000004U)  /* bit 2 */
#define ADC_CTRL1_AWDCH_3                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000008U)  /* bit 3 */
#define ADC_CTRL1_AWDCH_4                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000010U)  /* bit 4 */

#define ADC_CTRL1_ENDCIEN                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000020U)  /* bit 5 */

#define ADC_CTRL1_AWDIEN                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000040U)  /* bit 6 */

#define ADC_CTRL1_JENDCIEN                                     ((uint32_t)0x00000080U)  /* bit 7 */

#define ADC_CTRL1_SCANMD                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000100U)  /* bit 8 */

#define ADC_CTRL1_AWDSGLEN                                     ((uint32_t)0x00000200U)  /* bit 9 */

#define ADC_CTRL1_AUTOJC                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000400U)  /* bit 10 */

#define ADC_CTRL1_DREGCH                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000800U)  /* bit 11 */

#define ADC_CTRL1_DJCH                                         ((uint32_t)0x00001000U)  /* bit 12 */

#define ADC_CTRL1_DCTU                                         ((uint32_t)0x0000E000U)  /* bits [15:13] */
#define ADC_CTRL1_DCTU_MASK                                    ((uint32_t)0x0000E000U)  /* bits [15:13] field mask */
#define ADC_CTRL1_DCTU_0                                       ((uint32_t)0x00002000U)  /* bit 13 */
#define ADC_CTRL1_DCTU_1                                       ((uint32_t)0x00004000U)  /* bit 14 */
#define ADC_CTRL1_DCTU_2                                       ((uint32_t)0x00008000U)  /* bit 15 */

#define ADC_CTRL1_AWDEJCH                                      ((uint32_t)0x00400000U)  /* bit 22 */

#define ADC_CTRL1_AWDERCH                                      ((uint32_t)0x00800000U)  /* bit 23 */


/******** Bit definition for ADC_CTRL2 register ********/
#define ADC_CTRL2_ON                                           ((uint32_t)0x00000001U)  /* bit 0 */

#define ADC_CTRL2_CTU                                          ((uint32_t)0x00000002U)  /* bit 1 */

#define ADC_CTRL2_ENCAL                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000004U)  /* bit 2 */

#define ADC_CTRL2_DMAEN                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000100U)  /* bit 8 */

#define ADC_CTRL2_ALIG                                         ((uint32_t)0x00000800U)  /* bit 11 */

#define ADC_CTRL2_EXTJSEL                                      ((uint32_t)0x00007000U)  /* bits [14:12] */
#define ADC_CTRL2_EXTJSEL_MASK                                 ((uint32_t)0x00007000U)  /* bits [14:12] field mask */
#define ADC_CTRL2_EXTJSEL_0                                    ((uint32_t)0x00001000U)  /* bit 12 */
#define ADC_CTRL2_EXTJSEL_1                                    ((uint32_t)0x00002000U)  /* bit 13 */
#define ADC_CTRL2_EXTJSEL_2                                    ((uint32_t)0x00004000U)  /* bit 14 */

#define ADC_CTRL2_JTRIG                                        ((uint32_t)0x00008000U)  /* bit 15 */

#define ADC_CTRL2_EXTRSEL                                      ((uint32_t)0x000E0000U)  /* bits [19:17] */
#define ADC_CTRL2_EXTRSEL_MASK                                 ((uint32_t)0x000E0000U)  /* bits [19:17] field mask */
#define ADC_CTRL2_EXTRSEL_0                                    ((uint32_t)0x00020000U)  /* bit 17 */
#define ADC_CTRL2_EXTRSEL_1                                    ((uint32_t)0x00040000U)  /* bit 18 */
#define ADC_CTRL2_EXTRSEL_2                                    ((uint32_t)0x00080000U)  /* bit 19 */

#define ADC_CTRL2_TRIG                                         ((uint32_t)0x00100000U)  /* bit 20 */

#define ADC_CTRL2_SWSTRJCH                                     ((uint32_t)0x00200000U)  /* bit 21 */

#define ADC_CTRL2_SWSTRRCH                                     ((uint32_t)0x00400000U)  /* bit 22 */

#define ADC_CTRL2_TEMPEN                                       ((uint32_t)0x00800000U)  /* bit 23 */

#define ADC_CTRL2_VREFINT_TS_SEL                               ((uint32_t)0x01000000U)  /* bit 24 */


/******** Bit definition for ADC_SAMPT1 register ********/
#define ADC_SAMPT1_SAMP0                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000007U)  /* bits [2:0] */
#define ADC_SAMPT1_SAMP0_MASK                                  ((uint32_t)0x00000007U)  /* bits [2:0] field mask */
#define ADC_SAMPT1_SAMP0_0                                     ((uint32_t)0x00000001U)  /* bit 0 */
#define ADC_SAMPT1_SAMP0_1                                     ((uint32_t)0x00000002U)  /* bit 1 */
#define ADC_SAMPT1_SAMP0_2                                     ((uint32_t)0x00000004U)  /* bit 2 */

#define ADC_SAMPT1_SAMP1                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000038U)  /* bits [5:3] */
#define ADC_SAMPT1_SAMP1_MASK                                  ((uint32_t)0x00000038U)  /* bits [5:3] field mask */
#define ADC_SAMPT1_SAMP1_0                                     ((uint32_t)0x00000008U)  /* bit 3 */
#define ADC_SAMPT1_SAMP1_1                                     ((uint32_t)0x00000010U)  /* bit 4 */
#define ADC_SAMPT1_SAMP1_2                                     ((uint32_t)0x00000020U)  /* bit 5 */

#define ADC_SAMPT1_SAMP2                                       ((uint32_t)0x000001C0U)  /* bits [8:6] */
#define ADC_SAMPT1_SAMP2_MASK                                  ((uint32_t)0x000001C0U)  /* bits [8:6] field mask */
#define ADC_SAMPT1_SAMP2_0                                     ((uint32_t)0x00000040U)  /* bit 6 */
#define ADC_SAMPT1_SAMP2_1                                     ((uint32_t)0x00000080U)  /* bit 7 */
#define ADC_SAMPT1_SAMP2_2                                     ((uint32_t)0x00000100U)  /* bit 8 */

#define ADC_SAMPT1_SAMP3                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000E00U)  /* bits [11:9] */
#define ADC_SAMPT1_SAMP3_MASK                                  ((uint32_t)0x00000E00U)  /* bits [11:9] field mask */
#define ADC_SAMPT1_SAMP3_0                                     ((uint32_t)0x00000200U)  /* bit 9 */
#define ADC_SAMPT1_SAMP3_1                                     ((uint32_t)0x00000400U)  /* bit 10 */
#define ADC_SAMPT1_SAMP3_2                                     ((uint32_t)0x00000800U)  /* bit 11 */

#define ADC_CALFACT_CALFACTS_5                                 ((uint32_t)0x00000020U)  /* bit 5 */
#define ADC_CALFACT_CALFACTS_6                                 ((uint32_t)0x00000040U)  /* bit 6 */

#define ADC_CALFACT_CALFACTD                                   ((uint32_t)0x007F0000U)  /* bits [22:16] */
#define ADC_CALFACT_CALFACTD_MASK                              ((uint32_t)0x007F0000U)  /* bits [22:16] field mask */
#define ADC_CALFACT_CALFACTD_0                                 ((uint32_t)0x00010000U)  /* bit 16 */
#define ADC_CALFACT_CALFACTD_1                                 ((uint32_t)0x00020000U)  /* bit 17 */
#define ADC_CALFACT_CALFACTD_2                                 ((uint32_t)0x00040000U)  /* bit 18 */
#define ADC_CALFACT_CALFACTD_3                                 ((uint32_t)0x00080000U)  /* bit 19 */
#define ADC_CALFACT_CALFACTD_4                                 ((uint32_t)0x00100000U)  /* bit 20 */
#define ADC_CALFACT_CALFACTD_5                                 ((uint32_t)0x00200000U)  /* bit 21 */
#define ADC_CALFACT_CALFACTD_6                                 ((uint32_t)0x00400000U)  /* bit 22 */


/******** Bit definition for ADC_CTRL3 register ********/
#define ADC_CTRL3_RES                                          ((uint32_t)0x00000003U)  /* bits [1:0] */
#define ADC_CTRL3_RES_MASK                                     ((uint32_t)0x00000003U)  /* bits [1:0] field mask */
#define ADC_CTRL3_RES_0                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000001U)  /* bit 0 */
#define ADC_CTRL3_RES_1                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000002U)  /* bit 1 */

#define ADC_CTRL3_CALDIF                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000004U)  /* bit 2 */

#define ADC_CTRL3_CALALD                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000008U)  /* bit 3 */

#define ADC_CTRL3_RDY                                          ((uint32_t)0x00000020U)  /* bit 5 */

#define ADC_CTRL3_PDRDY                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000040U)  /* bit 6 */

#define ADC_CTRL3_BPCAL                                        ((uint32_t)0x00000080U)  /* bit 7 */

#define ADC_CTRL3_ENDCAIEN                                     ((uint32_t)0x00000100U)  /* bit 8 */

#define ADC_CTRL3_JENDCAIEN                                    ((uint32_t)0x00000200U)  /* bit 9 */

#define ADC_CTRL3_DPWMOD                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000400U)  /* bit 10 */

#define ADC_CTRL3_OSR                                          ((uint32_t)0x0000F000U)  /* bits [15:12] */
#define ADC_CTRL3_OSR_MASK                                     ((uint32_t)0x0000F000U)  /* bits [15:12] field mask */
#define ADC_CTRL3_OSR_0                                        ((uint32_t)0x00001000U)  /* bit 12 */
#define ADC_CTRL3_OSR_1                                        ((uint32_t)0x00002000U)  /* bit 13 */
#define ADC_CTRL3_OSR_2                                        ((uint32_t)0x00004000U)  /* bit 14 */
#define ADC_CTRL3_OSR_3                                        ((uint32_t)0x00008000U)  /* bit 15 */

#define ADC_CTRL3_OSS                                          ((uint32_t)0x000F0000U)  /* bits [19:16] */
#define ADC_CTRL3_OSS_MASK                                     ((uint32_t)0x000F0000U)  /* bits [19:16] field mask */
#define ADC_CTRL3_OSS_0                                        ((uint32_t)0x00010000U)  /* bit 16 */
#define ADC_CTRL3_OSS_1                                        ((uint32_t)0x00020000U)  /* bit 17 */
#define ADC_CTRL3_OSS_2                                        ((uint32_t)0x00040000U)  /* bit 18 */
#define ADC_CTRL3_OSS_3                                        ((uint32_t)0x00080000U)  /* bit 19 */

#define ADC_CTRL3_OSRE                                         ((uint32_t)0x00100000U)  /* bit 20 */

#define ADC_CTRL3_OSJE                                         ((uint32_t)0x00200000U)  /* bit 21 */

#define ADC_CTRL3_OSRTRIG                                      ((uint32_t)0x00400000U)  /* bit 22 */

#define ADC_CTRL3_OSRMD                                        ((uint32_t)0x00800000U)  /* bit 23 */

#define ADC_CTRL3_BUFF_EN                                      ((uint32_t)0x01000000U)  /* bit 24 */


/******** Bit definition for ADC_SAMPT3 register ********/
#define ADC_SAMPT3_SMPSEL                                      ((uint32_t)0x00000008U)  /* bit 3 */


/******** Bit definition for ADC_PCTRL register ********/
#define ADC_PCTRL_VREFLDOEN                                    ((uint32_t)0x00000008U)  /* bit 3 */

#define ADC_PCTRL_RANGESEL                                     ((uint32_t)0x00000010U)  /* bit 4 */

#define ADC_PCTRL_EXTPEN                                       ((uint32_t)0x00000020U)  /* bit 5 */

/***********************  Trim data struct   *******************/
typedef struct {
    uint32_t stote_bg_vtrim_value;
    uint32_t stote_rc28800_trim_value;
    uint32_t stote_rc32000_trim_value;
    uint32_t stote_rc32768_trim_value;
    uint32_t stote_rc64m_trim_value;
    uint32_t stote_rc96m_trim_value;
    uint8_t flash_uuid[16];
    uint32_t reserved0;
    uint32_t reserved1;
} trim_stored_t;
extern trim_stored_t* SystemTrimValueGet(void);
extern uint8_t* SystemGetUUID(void);
extern uint8_t* SystemGetMacAddr(void);

#ifdef __cplusplus
}
#endif

#endif /* __N32WB033_H__ */
